+create应用错误global type gf_apperror from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function integer gf_apperror ()
end prototypes

global function integer gf_apperror ();//==============================================================================
// 名称: Function - gf_apperror
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 功能: 应用错误
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 参数: 
// 		1.Integer ai_sysid	<说明>
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 返回: Integer 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 作者: Sunlf		日期: 2001.12.12
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 修改:    		日期: 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 备注: 
//==============================================================================

String   ls_ErrInfo
u_Transaction lo_Transaction
ls_ErrInfo = "1.代号：" + String(Error.Number) + "~n" + &
				 "2.信息：" + Error.Text + "~n" + &
				 "3.对象：" + Error.WindowMenu + "~n" + &
				 "4.控件：" + Error.Object + "~n" + &
				 "5.脚本：" + Error.ObjectEvent + "~n" + &
				 "6.行号：" + "第 " + String(Error.Line) + " 行"
ls_ErrInfo =ls_ErrInfo + "~r~n7.当前路径=" + GetCurrentDirectory()

if IsValid(gn_rtx) then 
	gn_rtx.uf_sendmsg(gs_rtxserver,'信息技术科',& 	'新门诊错误信息', ls_ErrInfo)
end if

IF sqlca.DBHandle() <= 0 THEN GOTO Process_WriteFile		// 数据库未连接

//==============================================================================
// 为防止系统非法操作事务不能回滚，不管有没有事务发生均执行Rollback函数
//==============================================================================
lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(sqlca)

//==============================================================================
// 在数据库中写碊AT*

//==============================================================================
gf_InsertErrLog(Error.Number,Error.Text,Error.WindowMenu,Error.Object,&                 Error.Objectevent,Error.Line)

//GOTO Process_ShowError

//==============================================================================
// 在文件中写错误日志
//==============================================================================
Process_WriteFile:

Integer li_FileNum 

li_FileNum = FileOpen("AppError.txt",LineMode!,Write!,LockWrite!,Append!)

FileWrite(li_FileNum,"")
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"//==============================================================================")
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"// " + String(Today(),"yyyy.mm.dd") + " " + String(Now(),"hh:mm:ss"))
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"//------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   1.代号：" + String(Error.Number))
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   2.信息：" + Error.Text)
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   3.对象：" + Error.WindowMenu)
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   4.控件：" + Error.Object)
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   5.脚本：" + Error.ObjectEvent)
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   6.行号：" + "第 " + String(Error.Line) + " 行")
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"   7.当前路径=" + string(GetCurrentDirectory())  )
FileWrite(li_FileNum,"//==============================================================================")

FileClose(li_FileNum)

//==============================================================================
// 提示错误信息
//==============================================================================
//Process_ShowError:
ls_ErrInfo = "~n系统发生错误，请记录以下错误信息后联系管理员处理。~n~n" + ls_ErrInfo + &
				 "~n~n是否退出系统?"



IF MessageBox("提示信息",ls_ErrInfo,StopSign!,YesNo!,1) = 1 THEN
	HALT CLOSE
END IF

RETURN 1

end function

BCtransaction.SB
取最大值global type gf_get_max from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function decimal gf_get_max (ref transaction ao_environment, string as_tablename, long an_needcount)
end prototypes

global function decimal gf_get_max (ref transaction ao_environment, string as_tablename, long an_needcount);Decimal{0} ll_Value
Long ll_Count
Long ll_Retry

u_Transaction lo_Transaction

IF IsNull(an_NeedCount) THEN RETURN -1
IF an_NeedCount < 1     THEN RETURN -1

as_tablename = Upper(as_tablename)
Process_Begin:
lo_Transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(ao_Environment)

UPDATE GY_IDENTITY SET VALUE = VALUE + :an_NeedCount
 WHERE TNAME = :as_TableName USING ao_Environment;

IF ao_Environment.sqlcode <> 0 THEN  // update失败，判断是否存在记录
	SELECT COUNT(*) INTO :ll_Count FROM GY_IDENTITY 
	 WHERE TNAME = :as_TableName USING ao_Environment;
	IF ll_Count > 0 THEN              // 记录存在继续update(最多三次)
		ll_Retry ++
		IF ll_Retry >= 3 THEN
			GOTO Process_Error
		ELSE
			lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(ao_Environment)
			GOTO Process_Begin
		END IF	
	ELSE                              // 记录不存在则插入新记录
		INSERT INTO GY_IDENTITY(TNAME,VALUE,ORIGIN_VALUE,INC_VALUE) 
		VALUES (:as_TableName,:an_NeedCount,1,1) USING ao_Environment;
		IF ao_Environment.SQLCode <> 0 THEN GOTO Process_Error
		ll_Value = an_NeedCount
		GOTO Process_Success
	END IF	
ELSE
	SELECT COUNT(*) INTO :ll_Count FROM GY_IDENTITY 
	WHERE TNAME = :as_TableName USING ao_Environment;
	IF ll_Count > 0 THEN              
	ELSE                              // 记录不存在则插入新记录
		INSERT INTO GY_IDENTITY(TNAME,VALUE,ORIGIN_VALUE,INC_VALUE) 
		VALUES (:as_TableName,:an_NeedCount,1,1) USING ao_Environment;
		IF ao_Environment.SQLCode <> 0 THEN GOTO Process_Error
		ll_Value = an_NeedCount
		GOTO Process_Success
	END IF	
END IF

SetNull(ll_Value)
SELECT VALUE INTO :ll_Value FROM GY_IDENTITY 
 WHERE TNAME = :as_TableName USING ao_Environment;

IF IsNull(ll_Value)        THEN GOTO Process_Error
IF ll_Value < an_NeedCount THEN GOTO Process_Error

Process_Success:
lo_Transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(ao_Environment)
ll_Value = ll_Value - an_NeedCount + 1
RETURN ll_Value

Process_Error:
lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(ao_Environment)

RETURN -1

end function

global type gf_get_ygzsm from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function string gf_get_ygzsm (string as_zsm, ref string as_ygzsm)
end prototypes

global function string gf_get_ygzsm (string as_zsm, ref string as_ygzsm);//====================================================================
// 事件: .gf_get_ygzsm()
//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// 描述:获得员工代码
//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// 参数:
// 	value	string	as_zsm	
//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// 返回:  string
//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// 作者:	linzr		日期: 2011.11.16
//====================================================================

String ls_ygdm,ls_ygzsm
SELECT ygzsm,ygdm Into :as_ygzsm,:ls_ygdm From gy_ygdm Where (ygzsm = :as_zsm Or ygdm = :as_zsm) AND ZFPB = 0 ;

if isnull(ls_ygdm) then ls_ygdm=''
if ls_ygdm = '' then ls_ygdm = as_zsm

RETURN ls_ygdm

end function

global function string gf_getinitpw (integer ai_xtsb, string as_yhdm);//==============================================================================
// 名称: Function - gf_getinitpw
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 功能: 获取系统初始化密码
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 参数: 
// 		1.Integer ai_xtsb	系统识别
// 		2.String as_yhdm	用户代码
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 返回: String 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 作者: Sunlf		日期: 2002.03.12
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 修改:    		日期: 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 备注: 
//==============================================================================

String	ls_InitPW

SELECT YHKL INTO :ls_InitPW FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
 WHERE XTSB = :ai_xtsb AND YHDM = :as_yhdm ;

ls_InitPW = Trim(ls_InitPW)

IF IsNull(ls_InitPW) THEN ls_InitPW = ""

RETURN ls_InitPW

end function

forward prototypes
global function string gf_getpara (integer li_xtsb, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz)
end prototypes

global function string gf_getpara (integer li_xtsb, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz);//*************************************************************************************//
// Function  : 得到系统某一参数设置
//
// Parameter : li_xtsb      系统标志
//             ls_csmc      参数名称
//             ls_default   默认值
//             ls_bz        备注
//
// Return    : 参数值
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2001.5.23
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//*************************************************************************************//

u_Transaction lo_Transaction
string ls_paraname,ls_valule

ls_paraname=upper(ls_csmc)

select CSZ into :ls_valule from GY_XTCS where XTSB=:li_xtsb and CSMC=:ls_paraname;

IF sqlca.sqlcode <> 0 THEN
	lo_Transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(sqlca)
	
	insert into  GY_XTCS(XTSB,CSMC,CSZ,BZ,MRZ) 
	              values(:li_xtsb,:ls_paraname,:ls_default,:ls_bz,:ls_default) ;
					  
	IF sqlca.sqlcode = 0 THEN
		lo_Transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(sqlca)
	ELSE
		lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(sqlca)
	END IF	
	
	ls_valule=ls_default
	
end if

IF IsNull(ls_valule) THEN ls_valule = ""

return ls_valule

end function

global function string gf_getpara_eas (transaction atr_transaction, integer li_xtsb, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz)
end prototypes

global function string gf_getpara_eas (transaction atr_transaction, integer li_xtsb, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz);//*************************************************************************************//
// Function  : 得到系统某一参数设置
//
// Parameter : atr_Transaction  事务
//					li_xtsb      系统标志
//             ls_csmc      参数名称
//             ls_default   默认值
//             ls_bz        备注
//
// Return    : 参数值

//*************************************************************************************//


string ls_paraname,ls_valule
if not isvalid(atr_Transaction) then
//	gf_rtxmsg("取系统参数失败：atr_Transaction 无效")
//	if IsValid(gn_rtx) then 
//		gn_rtx.uf_sendmsg(gs_rtxserver,'信息技术科',&
//		'新门诊错误信息', ls_ErrInfo)
//	end if

	return ""
end if
ls_paraname=upper(ls_csmc)

select CSZ into :ls_valule from GY_XTCS 
where XTSB=:li_xtsb and CSMC=:ls_paraname using atr_Transaction;

IF atr_Transaction.sqlcode =100 THEN
	
	
	insert into  GY_XTCS(XTSB,CSMC,CSZ,BZ,MRZ) 
	              values(:li_xtsb,:ls_paraname,:ls_default,:ls_bz,:ls_default)
	using atr_Transaction;
					  
	IF atr_Transaction.sqlcode = 0 THEN
		Commit using atr_Transaction;
	ELSE
		Rollback using atr_Transaction;
	END IF	
	
	ls_valule=ls_default
elseIF atr_Transaction.sqlcode =-1 THEN
//	gf_rtxmsg("取系统参数失败：" + atr_Transaction.sqlerrtext)
	ls_valule=''
end if

IF IsNull(ls_valule) THEN ls_valule = ""

return ls_valule

end function

global function string gf_getpara_jq (string as_jqmc, string as_csmc, string as_default);
u_Transaction lo_Transaction
string ls_valule

as_csmc=upper(as_csmc)
as_jqmc=Upper(as_jqmc)

select csz into :ls_valule from gy_jqcssz where jqmc=:as_jqmc and csmc=:as_csmc;

IF sqlca.sqlcode <> 0 THEN
	lo_Transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(sqlca)
	
	insert into  gy_jqcssz(jqmc,csmc,csz) 
	              values(:as_jqmc,:as_csmc,:as_default) ;
					  
	IF sqlca.sqlcode = 0 THEN
		lo_Transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(sqlca)
	ELSE
		lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(sqlca)
	END IF	
	ls_valule=as_default
	
end if

IF IsNull(ls_valule) THEN ls_valule = ""

return ls_valule

end function

global function string gf_getparan (integer li_xtsb, string as_csdm, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz)

global function string gf_getparan (integer li_xtsb, string as_csdm, string ls_csmc, string ls_default, string ls_bz);//功能：得到系统某一设置参数
//调用: gf_getpara(系统识别号,参数名称代码,默认值)
//例如: gf_getpara(1,"YJSJ","32")
//返回: 参数值
//作者: XUMS
string ls_paraname,ls_valule
ls_paraname=upper(ls_csmc)
select CSZ into :ls_valule from GY_XTCS_N where XTSB=:li_xtsb and CSDM =:as_csdm AND CSMC=:ls_paraname;
if isnull(ls_valule) or ls_valule = "" then
	gf_begin_transaction(sqlca)
	insert into  GY_XTCS_N(XTSB,CSDM,CSMC,CSZ,BZ,MRZ) values(:li_xtsb,:as_csdm,:ls_paraname,:ls_default,:ls_bz,:ls_default) ;
	gf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
	ls_valule=ls_default
end if
return ls_valule
end function

global type gf_getpym from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function string gf_getpym (string as_mc)
end prototypes

global function string gf_getpym (string as_mc);Integer li_i,li_asc
String s1,hz,pym,bm_py
li_i = 1
bm_py = ''

s1 = as_mc
DO WHILE li_i <= Len(as_mc) AND li_i < 13
	s1 = Mid(as_mc,li_i,Len(as_mc))
	li_asc = Asc(s1)
	IF li_asc >= 176 THEN
		hz = Mid(as_mc,li_i,2)
		pym = ''
		SELECT PY  INTO :pym  FROM GY_HZBM Where hz = :hz;
		li_i += 2
		bm_py += pym
		
	ELSE
		IF (li_asc >= 48 AND li_asc <= 57) OR &
			(li_asc >= 65 AND li_asc <= 90) OR 	&
			(li_asc >= 97 AND li_asc <= 122) THEN
			bm_py += Mid(as_mc,li_i,1)
			
			li_i ++
		ELSEIF li_asc>=129 and li_asc<176 THEN
			li_i = li_i + 2
		ELSE
			li_i++
		END IF
	END IF
LOOP

RETURN Upper(bm_py)


end function

取用户姓名global type gf_getusername from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function string gf_getusername (integer ai_xtsb, string as_yhdm)
end prototypes

global function string gf_getusername (integer ai_xtsb, string as_yhdm);//*******************************************************************************//
// Function  : 取得用户名称
//
// Parameter : ai_xtsb  系统标志  as_yhdm 用户代码
//
// Return    : String 返回用户名称
//
// Author    : XUMS
// Created   : 
// Modified  : 2002.04.23
// Log       : 
// Memo      :系统标志=0,表示忽略系统标志
//*******************************************************************************//

string ls_name

if ai_xtsb=0 then 
	select YHMC into :ls_name from SS_OPERATE_USER  WHERE YHDM=:as_yhdm;
else
	select YHMC into :ls_name from SS_OPERATE_USER  WHERE YHDM=:as_yhdm and XTSB=:ai_xtsb;	
end if	
if isnull(ls_name) then ls_name=""

return ls_name
end function

global function integer gf_insert_xtrz (string as_rzlx, string as_xtbb, string as_bzxx);String ls_computername
ls_computername = uo_support.getcomputername()
gdt_sysdatetime = gf_server_date()

gf_begin_transaction(sqlca)
INSERT INTO "SS_XTRZ"
	( "XTSB",              "JQM",              "JLSJ",              "RZLX", 	"CZGH",              "XTBB",              "BZXX" )
	VALUES ( :base_info.syscode, :ls_computername, :gdt_sysdatetime,:as_rzlx, 	:base_info.UserID,:as_xtbb,:as_bzxx )  ;
	
IF Pos(sqlca.SQLErrText,"not connected") > 0 And as_rzlx = 'XTTC' THEN
	CONNECT Using sqlca;
	IF sqlca.SQLCode <> 0 THEN
		
	ELSE
		INSERT INTO "SS_XTRZ"
			( "XTSB",              "JQM",              "JLSJ",              "RZLX", 			"CZGH",              "XTBB",              "BZXX" )
			VALUES ( :base_info.syswebloginsoap nonvisualobject accessiblestate accessibility

http://schemas.xmlsoap.org写错误日志global type gf_inserterrlog from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function integer gf_inserterrlog (integer no, string text, string window, string object, string objectevent, integer row)
end prototypes

global function integer gf_inserterrlog (integer no, string text, string window, string object, string objectevent, integer row);//功能:写错识日志库
//调用:gf_inserterrlog(错误号，错误信息，窗口，对象，事件，行号)
if IsValid(uo_support) = false then return 1
		transaction sqlca2
		datetime ldt_date
		long li_systemid
		string ls_userid
		li_systemid=uo_loginclass.getsystemid()
		ls_userid=uo_loginclass.getuserid()
		ldt_date=gf_server_date()

		sqlca2 = create transaction

		sqlca2.DBMS 		= sqlca.DBMS
		sqlca2.servername = sqlca.servername
		sqlca2.database	= sqlca.database  
		sqlca2.logid		= sqlca.logid     
		sqlca2.logpass		= sqlca.logpass   
		sqlca2.userid		= sqlca.userid    
		sqlca2.dbpass		= sqlca.dbpass    
		sqlca2.dbparm		= sqlca.dbparm    
		sqlca2.lock			= sqlca.lock	   

		CONNECT USING sqlca2;
		if sqlca2.sqlcode <> 0 then return 0
	
		insert into SS_ERRORLOG(SYSTEMID,ERROR_DATE,USER_ID,ERROR_NO,TEXT,WINDOW,OBJECT,EVENT,ROW)
		      values(:li_systemid,:ldt_date,:ls_userid,:no,:text,:window,:object,:objectevent,:row) USING sqlca2;
		commit using sqlca2;
		disconnect using sqlca2;
		destroy sqlca2
	return 1	
end function

global type gf_insertlog from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function integer gf_insertlog (string modelname, string infotext)
end prototypes

global function integer gf_insertlog (string modelname, string infotext);//功能:写日志库
//调用:gf_log(模块名称，文本，类型，网卡地址)
		transaction sqlca2
		datetime ldt_date
		long li_systemid
		string ls_userid,ls_netaddress
		integer li_xxbh
		li_systemid=uo_loginclass.getsystemid()
		ls_userid=uo_loginclass.getuserid()
		ldt_date=gf_server_date()
		ls_netaddress=uo_support.getcomputername()
		select max(XXBH) into :li_xxbh from SS_LOG_INFO where XTSB=:li_systemid;
		if li_xxbh=0 or isnull(li_xxbh) then 
			li_xxbh=1
		else
			li_xxbh=li_xxbh+1
		end if

		sqlca2 = create transaction

		sqlca2.DBMS 		= sqlca.DBMS
		sqlca2.servername = sqlca.servername
		sqlca2.database	= sqlca.database  
		sqlca2.logid		= sqlca.logid     
		sqlca2.logpass		= sqlca.logpass   
		sqlca2.userid		= sqlca.userid    
		sqlca2.dbpass		= sqlca.dbpass    
		sqlca2.dbparm		= sqlca.dbparm    
		sqlca2.lock			= sqlca.lock	   

		CONNECT USING sqlca2;
		if sqlca2.sqlcode <> 0 then return 0
	
		insert into SS_LOG(XTSB,XXBH,JLSJ,YHBH,MKMC,JLWB,WKDZ)
		      values(:li_systemid,:li_xxbh,:ldt_date,:ls_userid,:modelname,:infotext,:ls_netaddress) USING sqlca2;
		insert into SS_LOG_INFO(XTSB,XXBH,XXJB,XXNR)
		      values(:li_systemid,:li_xxbh,'I',:infotext) USING sqlca2;				
		commit using sqlca2;
		disconnect using sqlca2;
		destroy sqlca2
	return 1	
end function

设置系统初始化完成标志global type gf_setinitflag from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function boolean gf_setinitflag (integer ai_xtsb, string as_yhdm, string as_flag)
end prototypes

global function boolean gf_setinitflag (integer ai_xtsb, string as_yhdm, string as_flag);//==============================================================================
// 名称: Function - gf_setinitflag
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 功能: 设置系统初始化完成标志
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 参数: 
// 		1.Integer ai_xtsb	<说明>
// 		2.String as_yhdm	<说明>
// 		3.String as_flag	<说明>
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 返回: Boolean 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 作者: Sunlf		日期: 2002.03.13
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 修改:    		日期: 
//------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 备注: 
//==============================================================================

String	ls_InitFlag
DateTime	ldt_SysDate

SetNull(ls_InitFlag)

IF as_Flag = ""         THEN as_Flag = "1"
IF Pos(as_Flag,"[") = 0 THEN as_Flag = "[" + Trim(as_Flag) + "]"

SELECT CSHF INTO :ls_InitFlag FROM SS_INSTALL WHERE XTSB = :ai_xtsb ;

IF IsNull(ls_InitFlag) THEN ls_InitFlag = ""

ls_InitFlag = Trim(ls_InitFlag) + as_Flag

ldt_SysDate =gf_server_date()

UPDATE SS_INSTALL SET CSHF = :ls_InitFlag, CSGH = :as_yhdm, CSRQ = :ldt_SysDate 
 WHERE XTSB = :ai_xtsb ;

RETURN sqlca.sqlcode = 0

end function

设置参数global type gf_setpara from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function boolean gf_setpara (ref transaction ao_environment, integer an_systemid, string as_paraname, string as_value, string as_comment)
end prototypes

global function boolean gf_setpara (ref transaction ao_environment, integer an_systemid, string as_paraname, string as_value, string as_comment);as_ParaName = Upper(as_ParaName)

u_Transaction lo_Transaction

lo_Transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(ao_Environment)

Insert Into GY_XTCS(XTSB,CSMC,CSZ,BZ,MRZ) 
	Values(:an_SystemID,:as_ParaName,:as_Value,:as_Comment,:as_Value) Using ao_Environment;
	
if ao_Environment.SQLCode=0 then
	lo_Transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(ao_Environment)
	return True
else
	lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(ao_Environment)
end if 

lo_Transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(ao_Environment)

Update GY_XTCS Set CSZ=:as_Value,BZ=:as_Comment
	Where XTSB=:an_SystemID And CSMC=:as_ParaName Using ao_Environment;
	
if ao_Environment.SQLCode=0 then
	lo_Transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(ao_Environment)
	return True
else
	lo_Transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(ao_Environment)
end if 



return False

end function

global function boolean gf_setparan (ref transaction ao_environment, integer an_systemid, string as_csdm, string as_paraname, string as_value, string as_comment)
end prototypes

global function boolean gf_setparan (ref transaction ao_environment, integer an_systemid, string as_csdm, string as_paraname, string as_value, string as_comment);as_ParaName = Upper(as_ParaName)
Insert Into GY_XTCS_N(XTSB,CSDM,CSMC,CSZ,BZ,MRZ) 
	Values(:an_SystemID,:as_csdm,:as_ParaName,:as_Value,:as_Comment,:as_Value) Using ao_Environment;
if ao_Environment.SQLCode=0 then return True

Update GY_XTCS_N Set CSZ=:as_Value,BZ=:as_Comment
	Where XTSB=:an_SystemID AND CSDM =:as_csdm And CSMC=:as_ParaName Using ao_Environment;
if ao_Environment.SQLCode=0 then return True
return False

end function

public function boolean opendialog ();
/*
This function displays the open dialog box available in the COMDLG32 library..

Members available:

string Filter			=> This member allows to specify a list of tab separated filters;
integer FilterIndex	=> Default filter index
string InitialDir		=> Default directory (for the dialog box)
string Title			=> Dialog title
long Flags				=> Dialog Flags
string Filename 		=> returns selected file
string Files[]			=> Array with the list of selected files
string PathName		=> Path of the files
integer FileCount		=> Number of selected files
*/

OPENFILENAME OpenFileName
string ls_Token, Empty[]
integer li_TabPos, li_Start

OpenFileName.lStructSize	= 76
OpenFileName.hWndOwner		= hWndParent
OpenFileName.hInstance		= 0
OpenFileName.lpstrFilter	= LocalAlloc(LMEM_ZEROINIT,Len(Filter) + 2) // 2 nulls to signal end
If OpenFileName.lpstrFilter = 0 Then
	MessageBox("Error","Cannot alloc requested memory!",StopSign!,Ok!)
	Return(False)
End If

// Tab separator to Null separator 
li_Start  = 1
li_TabPos = Pos(Filter,"~t",1)
Do While li_TabPos > 0
	ls_Token = Mid(Filter,li_Start,li_TabPos - li_Start)
	StrCopy(OpenFileName.lpstrFilter + (li_Start - 1),ls_Token,Len(ls_Token))
	li_Start		= li_TabPos + 1
	li_TabPos	= Pos(Filter,"~t",li_TabPos + 1)
Loop
ls_Token	= Mid(Filter,li_Start)
StrCopy(OpenFileName.lpstrFilter + (li_Start - 1),ls_Token,Len(ls_Token))

OpenFileName.lpstrCustomFilter	= 0
OpenFileName.nMaxCustomFilter		= 0
OpenFileName.nFilterIndex 			= FilterIndex
OpenFileName.lpstrFile				= LocalAlloc(LMEM_ZEROINIT,MAXFILENAME * MAXFILES) 
OpenFileName.nMaxFile				= MAXFILENAME * MAXFILES
OpenFileName.lpstrFileTitle		= LocalAlloc(0,MAXFILENAME)
OpenFileName.nMaxFileTitle			= MAXFILENAME
OpenFileName.lpstrInitialDir		= LocalAlloc(0,MAXPATHNAME)
StrCopy(OpenFileName.lpstrInitialDir,InitialDir,Len(InitialDir))
OpenFileName.lpstrTitle				= LocalAlloc(0,255)
StrCopy(OpenFileName.lpstrTitle,Title,Len(Title))
OpenFileName.Flags					= Flags
OpenFileName.nFileOffSet			= 0
OpenFileName.nFileExtension		= 0
OpenFileName.lpstrDefExt			= 0
OpenFileName.lCustData				= 0
OpenFileName.lpfnHook				= 0
OpenFileName.lpTemplateName		= 0

Files = Empty // Reset array
FileCount = 0

If GetOpenFileNameA(OpenFileName) = 1 Then // Pressed OK button
	PathName = Left(String(OpenFileName.lpstrFile,"address"),OpenFileName.nFileOffSet - 1)
	li_Start = 0
	Do
		ls_Token = String(OpenFileName.lpstrFile + OpenFileName.nFileOffSet + li_Start,"address")
		If ls_Token <> "" Then
			FileCount++
			Files[FileCount] = ls_Token
		End If
		li_Start += Len(ls_Token) + 1
	Loop Until ls_Token = ""
	FileName = Files[1]
Else
	FileName  = ""
	FileCount = 0
End If

LocalFree(OpenFileName.lpstrFilter)
LocalFree(OpenFileName.lpstrFile)
LocalFree(OpenFileName.lpstrFileTitle)
LocalFree(OpenFileName.lpstrTitle)
LocalFree(OpenFileName.lpstrInitialDir)

Return(FileCount > 0)
end function

public function boolean printdialog ();/*

Shows the print dialog box... 

Members:

integer FromPage	// Print from page
integer ToPage		// Print to page
integer MinPage	// Min page (smaller page)
integer MaxPage	// Max page (higher page)
PROTECTEDWRITE integer Copies = 1 // N
 of copies to print...

*/

PRINTDLG PrintDlg
DEVMODE DevMode
DEVNAMES DevNames
long pDevNames, pDevMode

PrintDlg.lStructSize 	= 66
PrintDlg.hWndOwner		= hWndParent
PrintDlg.hDevMode			= 0
PrintDlg.hDevNames		= 0
PrintDlg.hDC				= 0
PrintDlg.Flags				= Flags
PrintDlg.nFromPage		= FromPage
PrintDlg.nToPage			= ToPage
PrintDlg.nMinPage			= MinPage
PrintDlg.nMaxPage			= MaxPage
PrintDlg.nCopies			= 0
PrintDlg.hInstance		= 0
PrintDlg.lCustData		= 0
PrintDlg.lpfnPrintHook	= 0
PrintDlg.lpfnSetupHook	= 0
PrintDlg.lpPrintTemplateName = 0
PrintDlg.lpSetupTemplateName = 0
PrintDlg.hPrintTemplate	= 0
PrintDlg.hSetupTemplate = 0

If PrintDlgA(PrintDlg) = 1 Then

	pDevMode = LocalLock(PrintDlg.hDevMode)
	GetDevMode(DevMode,pDevMode,148) // Lock dynamic memory handle
	LocalUnlock(pDevMode) // Unlock dynamic memory handle

	pDevNames = LocalLock(PrintDlg.hDevNames)
	GetDevNames(DevNames,pDevNames,8) // Lock dynamic memory handle
	LocalUnlock(pDevNames) // Unlock dynamic memory handle

	FromPage	= PrintDlg.nFromPage
	ToPage	= PrintDlg.nToPage
	MinPage 	= PrintDlg.nMinPage
	MaxPage 	= PrintDlg.nMaxPage
	Copies	= PrintDlg.nCopies
	
	If Copies = 1 Then // Copies are provided by devmode..
		Copies = DevMode.dmCopies
	End If

	SetPrinter(String(pDevNames + DevNames.wDeviceOffset,"address"), & 				  String(pDevNames + DevNames.wDriverOffset,"address"), & 				  String(pDevNames + DevNames.wOutPutOffset,"address"))

	Return(True)

End If	
	
Return(False)

end function

public subroutine setprinter (string printername, string driver, string port);// Function used to set the default printer based on the choice maded on the print 
// dialog box..
// Arguments:

// PrinterName => Name of the new printer
// Driver => Driver of the new printer
// Port => Port of the new printer

//Environment Env
string ls_WinDir

GetEnvironment(Uo_support.Env)

Choose Case Uo_support.Env.OSType
	Case Windows!
		RegistrySet("HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\Config\0001\System\CurrentControlSet\Control\Print\Printers", "Default", PrinterName)
		ls_WinDir = Space(260)
		GetWindowsDirectoryA(ls_WinDir,260) // GetWindows directory
		SetProfileString(ls_WinDir + "\WIN.INI","Windows","Device",PrinterName + "," + Driver + "," + Port)
	Case WindowsNT!
		RegistrySet("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\Software\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Windows","Device", PrinterName)
End Choose
end subroutine

public function boolean savedialog ();
/*
This function displays the save dialog box available in the COMDLG32 library..

Members available:

string Filter			=> This member allows to specify a list of tab separated filters;
integer FilterIndex	=> Default filter index (returns the selected filter)
string InitialDir		=> Default directory (for the dialog box)
string Title			=> Dialog title
long Flags				=> Dialog Flags
string Filename 		=> returns selected file
string Files[]			=> Array with the list of selected files
string PathName		=> Path of the files
integer FileCount		=> Number of selected files
*/

OPENFILENAME SaveFileName
string ls_Token, Filters[]
integer li_TabPos, li_Start

SaveFileName.lStructSize	= 76
SaveFileName.hWndOwner		= hWndParent
SaveFileName.hInstance		= 0
SaveFileName.lpstrFilter	= LocalAlloc(LMEM_ZEROINIT,Len(Filter) + 2) // 2 nulls to signal end
If SaveFileName.lpstrFilter = 0 Then
	MessageBox("Error","Cannot alloc requested memory!",StopSign!,Ok!)
	Return(False)
End If

// Tab separator to Null separator 
li_Start  = 1
li_TabPos = Pos(Filter,"~t",1)
Do While li_TabPos > 0
	ls_Token = Mid(Filter,li_Start,li_TabPos - li_Start)
	StrCopy(SaveFileName.lpstrFilter + (li_Start - 1),ls_Token,Len(ls_Token))
	li_Start		= li_TabPos + 1
	li_TabPos	= Pos(Filter,"~t",li_TabPos + 1)
	Filters[UpperBound(Filters) + 1] = Mid(ls_Token,2)
Loop
ls_Token	= Mid(Filter,li_Start)
StrCopy(SaveFileName.lpstrFilter + (li_Start - 1),ls_Token,Len(ls_Token))

SaveFileName.lpstrCustomFilter	= 0
SaveFileName.nMaxCustomFilter		= 0
SaveFileName.nFilterIndex 			= FilterIndex
SaveFileName.lpstrFile				= LocalAlloc(LMEM_ZEROINIT,MAXFILENAME) 
SaveFileName.nMaxFile				= MAXFILENAME
SaveFileName.lpstrFileTitle		= LocalAlloc(0,MAXFILENAME)
SaveFileName.nMaxFileTitle			= MAXFILENAME
SaveFileName.lpstrInitialDir		= LocalAlloc(0,MAXPATHNAME)
StrCopy(SaveFileName.lpstrInitialDir,InitialDir,Len(InitialDir))
SaveFileName.lpstrTitle				= LocalAlloc(0,255)
StrCopy(SaveFileName.lpstrTitle,Title,Len(Title))
SaveFileName.Flags					= Flags
SaveFileName.nFileOffSet			= 0
SaveFileName.nFileExtension		= 0
SaveFileName.lpstrDefExt			= 0
SaveFileName.lCustData				= 0
SaveFileName.lpfnHook				= 0
SaveFileName.lpTemplateName		= 0

If GetSaveFileNameA(SaveFileName) = 1 Then // Pressed OK button
	PathName = Left(String(SaveFileName.lpstrFile,"address"),SaveFileName.nFileOffSet - 1)
	FileName = String(SaveFileName.lpstrFile + SaveFileName.nFileOffSet,"address")
	If SaveFileName.nFileExtension = Len(PathName) + Len(FileName) + 1 Then
		FileName += Filters[2 * SaveFileName.nFilterIndex]
	End If
Else
	FileName  = ""
End If

LocalFree(SaveFileName.lpstrFilter)
LocalFree(SaveFileName.lpstrFile)
LocalFree(SaveFileName.lpstrFileTitle)
LocalFree(SaveFileName.lpstrTitle)
LocalFree(SaveFileName.lpstrInitialDir)

Return(FileName <> "")
end function

public function boolean fontdialog ();
/*
This function displays the font dialog box available in the COMDLG32 library..

Members:

integer FontType
integer SizeMin
integer SizeMax
string FaceName
long  Height
long  Weight
boolean Italic
boolean Underline
boolean StrikeOut

*/
CHOOSEFONT ChooseFont
LOGFONT	LogFont
boolean lb_Result

If Flags = 0 Then Flags = gf_BitOr({CF_FORCEFONTEXIST,CF_BOTH,CF_EFFECTS,CF_INITTOLOGFONTSTRUCT})

LogFont.lfFaceName	= FaceName
LogFont.lfHeight		= Height
LogFont.lfWeight		= Weight
If Italic Then LogFont.lfItalic = Char(255) Else LogFont.lfItalic = Char(0)
If Underline Then LogFont.lfUnderline = Char(1) Else LogFont.lfUnderline = Char(0)
If StrikeOut Then LogFont.lfStrikeOut = Char(1) Else LogFont.lfStrikeOut = Char(0)

ChooseFont.lStructSize	= 60
ChooseFont.hWndOwner		= hWndParent
ChooseFont.hDC				= 0
ChooseFont.lpLogFont		= LocalAlloc(0,60)
PutLogFont(ChooseFont.lpLogFont,LogFont,60)
ChooseFont.iPointSize	= 0
ChooseFont.Flags			= Flags
ChooseFont.RGBColors		= RGBColors
ChooseFont.lCustData		= 0
ChooseFont.lpfnHook		= 0
ChooseFont.lpTemplateName	= 0
ChooseFont.hInstance 	= 0
ChooseFont.lpszStyle 	= 0
ChooseFont.nFontType 	= FontType
ChooseFont.nSizeMin 		= SizeMin
ChooseFont.nSizeMax 		= SizeMax

If ChooseFontA(ChooseFont) = 1 Then
	GetLogFont(LogFont,ChooseFont.lpLogFont,60)

	FontType	= ChooseFont.nFontType
	FaceName	= LogFont.lfFaceName
	Height	= LogFont.lfHeight
	Weight	= LogFont.lfWeight
	Italic	= (Asc(LogFont.lfItalic) 	 = 255)
	Underline= (Asc(LogFont.lfUnderline) = 1)
	StrikeOut= (Asc(LogFont.lfStrikeOut) = 1)

	lb_Result = True
Else
	FontType	= 0
	FaceName	= ""
	Height	= 0
	Weight	= 0
	Italic	= False
	Underline= False
	StrikeOut= False
	lb_Result = False
End If

LocalFree(ChooseFont.lpLogFont)

Return(lb_Result)

end functi
on

TriggerEvent( this, "constructor" )
end on

on u_commdlg.destroy
TriggerEvent( this, "destructor" )
call super::destroy
end on

层次代码类（父类：u_CodeClass）forward
global type u_levelcodeclass from u_codeclass
end type
end forward

global type u_levelcodeclass from u_codeclass
end type

type variables
Protected:
String is_ParentColumn
s_NodeInfo ist_NodeInfo[]
TreeViewItem io_TreeItem
Boolean ib_CodeCoding  //代码列为字符编码

Private:
Long in_ChildAmount
//Boolean ib_CanWork
end variables

forward prototypes
public function boolean createtree (treeview ao_treeobject, string as_rootname)
public subroutine setparentcolumn (string as_parentname)
public function long getchild (string as_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref string as_childarray[])
public function long getchild (long an_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref long an_childarray[])
public function long getrootcode (long an_codevalue)
public function string getrootcode (string as_codevalue)
public function boolean ischild (long an_parent, long an_object, boolean ab_canself)
public function boolean ischild (string as_parent, string as_object, boolean ab_canself)
private subroutine childrecursiondecimal (long an_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref long an_childarray[])
private subroutine childrecursionstring (string as_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref string as_childarray[])
private subroutine createrecursion (treeview ao_treeobject, long an_currentrow)
end prototypes

public function boolean createtree (treeview ao_treeobject, string as_rootname);long ln_Circle
if in_ObjectRows<1 then return False
io_TreeItem.PictureIndex = 1
io_TreeItem.SelectedPictureIndex = 2
ao_TreeObject.SetRedraw(False)
ao_TreeObject.DeleteItem(1)  //删除所有元素
for ln_Circle=1 to in_ObjectRows
	ist_NodeInfo[ln_Circle].FilledFlag = False
next
io_TreeItem.Label = as_RootName
if IsDecimal() then 
	io_TreeItem.Data = 0
else
	io_TreeItem.Data = ""
end if
ao_TreeObject.InsertItemLast(0,io_TreeItem)  //插入根节点，句柄为1
for ln_Circle=1 to in_ObjectRows
	if ist_NodeInfo[ln_Circle].FilledFlag then continue  //此行信息已插入
	CreateRecursion(ao_TreeObject,ln_Circle)
next
ao_TreeObject.ExpandItem(1)
ao_TreeObject.SetRedraw(True)
return True

end function

public subroutine setparentcolumn (string as_parentname);is_ParentColumn = as_ParentName
return
end subroutine

public function long getchild (string as_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref string as_childarray[]);////得到给定元素的子元素函数//////
if (not(CanWork())) then return -1
in_ChildAmount = 0
if IsDecimal() then return -1
ChildRecursionString(as_CodeValue,ab_LeafOnly,as_ChildArray)
return in_ChildAmount

end function

public function long getchild (long an_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref long an_childarray[]);////得到给定元素的子元素函数//////
if (not(CanWork())) then return -1
in_ChildAmount = 0
if not IsDecimal() then return -1
ChildRecursionDecimal(an_CodeValue,ab_LeafOnly,an_ChildArray)
return in_ChildAmount

end function

public function long getrootcode (long an_codevalue);///////////取指定代码的根代码函数///////////
//参数1：指定代码值an_CodeValue           //
//返回：此代码的根代码（-1为出错）        //
////////////////////////////////////////////
long ln_FindRow,ln_CodeValue,ln_ParentValue
if not CanWork() then return -1
if not IsDecimal() then return -1
ln_CodeValue = an_CodeValue
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow = io_Object.Find(is_CodeColumn+"="+String(ln_CodeValue),1,in_ObjectRows)
	//未找到，返回出错
	if ln_FindRow<=0 then return -1 
	//取找到代码的父代码
	ln_ParentValue = io_Object.GetItemNumber(ln_FindRow,is_CodeColumn)
	if ln_ParentValue=ln_CodeValue then exit  //如父项目等于当前项目，则返回
	ln_CodeValue = ln_ParentValue  //不等，当前项目赋值为父项目
loop
return ln_CodeValue
end function

public function string getrootcode (string as_codevalue);///////////取指定代码的根代码函数///////////
//参数1：指定代码值as_CodeValue           //
//返回：此代码的根代码（-1为出错）        //
////////////////////////////////////////////
long ln_FindRow
//找到行
String ls_CodeValue,ls_ParentValue
//父代码，当前代码
if not CanWork() then return "Error"
if IsDecimal() then return "Error"
ls_CodeValue = Upper(as_CodeValue)
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow=io_Object.Find("Upper("+is_CodeColumn+")='"+ls_CodeValue+"'",1,in_ObjectRows)
	//未找到，返回出错
	if ln_FindRow<=0 then return "Error"
	//取找到代码的父代码
	ls_ParentValue = Upper(io_Object.GetItemString(ln_FindRow,is_CodeColumn))
	if ls_ParentValue=ls_CodeValue then exit  //如父项目等于当前项目，则返回
	ls_CodeValue = ls_ParentValue  //不等，当前项目赋值为父项目
loop
return ls_CodeValue
end function

public function boolean ischild (long an_parent, long an_object, boolean ab_canself);long ln_FindRow,ln_Parent,ln_Child
if in_ObjectRows<1 then return False
if (an_Parent=an_Object) then return ab_CanSelf  //自己为自己的子孙
ln_Child = an_Object
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow = io_Object.Find(is_CodeColumn+"="+String(ln_Child),1,in_ObjectRows)
	if ln_FindRow<1 then exit
	ln_Parent = io_Object.GetItemNumber(ln_FindRow,is_ParentColumn)
	if (ln_Parent=an_Parent) then return True
	if (ln_Parent=ln_Child) then return False
	ln_Child = ln_Parent
loop
return False

end function

public function boolean ischild (string as_parent, string as_object, boolean ab_canself);long ln_FindRow
String ls_Parent,ls_Child
if in_ObjectRows<1 then return False
if (as_Parent=as_Object) then return ab_CanSelf  //自己为自己的子孙
ls_Child = as_Object
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow = io_Object.Find("Upper("+is_CodeColumn+")='"+ls_Child+"'",1,in_ObjectRows)
	if ln_FindRow<1 then exit
	ls_Parent = io_Object.GetItemString(ln_FindRow,is_ParentColumn)
	if (ls_Parent=as_Parent) then return True
	if (ls_Parent=ls_Child) then return False
	ls_Child = ls_Parent
loop
return False

end function

private subroutine childrecursiondecimal (long an_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref long an_childarray[]);long ln_CurRow,ln_FindRow,ln_CodeValue,ln_CurChildAmount
ln_CurRow = 1
ln_CurChildAmount = in_ChildAmount
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow = io_Object.Find(is_ParentColumn+"="+String(an_CodeValue),& 		ln_CurRow,in_ObjectRows)
	if (ln_FindRow<1) then exit
	//找到子孙
	ln_CodeValue = io_Object.GetItemNumber(ln_FindRow,is_CodeColumn)
	ln_CurRow = ln_FindRow + 1
	if (ln_CodeValue=an_CodeValue) then continue  //元素自身
	ChildRecursionDecimal(ln_CodeValue,ab_LeafOnly,an_ChildArray)  //递归调用
	if (ln_CurChildAmount=in_ChildAmount)or(not(ab_LeafOnly)) then  //无子孙或允许插入非叶子
		in_ChildAmount++
		ln_CurChildAmount = in_ChildAmount
		an_ChildArray[in_ChildAmount] = ln_CodeValue		
	end if
	if (ln_FindRow=in_ObjectRows) then exit  //最后一行
loop
return 
end subroutine

private subroutine childrecursionstring (string as_codevalue, boolean ab_leafonly, ref string as_childarray[]);long ln_CurRow,ln_FindRow,ln_CurChildAmount
String ls_CodeValue
ln_CurRow = 1
ln_CurChildAmount = in_ChildAmount
do while (True)
	ln_FindRow = io_Object.Find(is_ParentColumn+"='"+as_CodeValue+"'",& 		ln_CurRow,in_ObjectRows)
	if (ln_FindRow<1) then exit
	//找到子孙
	ls_CodeValue = io_Object.GetItemString(ln_FindRow,is_CodeColumn)
	ln_CurRow = ln_FindRow + 1
	if (ls_CodeValue=as_CodeValue) then continue  //元素自身
	ChildRecursionString(ls_CodeValue,ab_LeafOnly,as_ChildArray)  //递归调用
	if (ln_CurChildAmount=in_ChildAmount)or(not(ab_LeafOnly)) then  //无子孙或允许插入非叶子
		in_ChildAmount++
		ln_CurChildAmount = in_ChildAmount
		as_ChildArray[in_ChildAmount] = ls_CodeValue		
	end if
	if (ln_FindRow=in_ObjectRows) then exit  //最后一行
loop
return 
end subroutine

private subroutine createrecursion (treeview ao_treeobject, long an_currentrow);//////递归生成树函数//////
long ln_ParentRow,ln_ItemHandle
Any la_CodeValue,la_ParentValue
if IsDecimal() then  //数值类型
	la_CodeValue = io_Object.GetItemNumber(an_CurrentRow,is_CodeColumn)
	la_ParentValue = io_Object.GetItemNumber(an_CurrentRow,is_ParentColumn)
else  //字符类型
	la_CodeValue = io_Object.GetItemString(an_CurrentRow,is_CodeColumn)
	la_ParentValue = io_Object.GetItemString(an_CurrentRow,is_ParentColumn)
end if
if (la_CodeValue<>la_ParentValue) then  //非根节点
	if IsDecimal() then
		ln_ParentRow=io_Object.Find(is_CodeColumn+"="+String(la_ParentValue),1,in_ObjectRows)
	else
		ln_ParentRow=io_Object.Find(is_CodeColumn+"='"+la_ParentValue+"'",1,in_ObjectRows)
	end if
	if (ln_ParentRow<1) then return	
	if (not(ist_NodeInfo[ln_ParentRow].FilledFlag)) then  //父节点未插入
		CreateRecursion(ao_TreeObject,ln_ParentRow)
	end if
else  //插入根节点
	io_TreeItem.Label = io_Object.GetItemString(an_CurrentRow,is_NameColumn)
	io_TreeItem.Data = la_CodeValue
	ln_ItemHandle = ao_TreeObject.InsertItemLast(1,io_TreeItem)
	ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].FilledFlag = True
	ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].ItemHandle = ln_ItemHandle
	ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].DataValue = la_CodeValue
	return
end if
//插入当前节点
io_TreeItem.Label = io_Object.GetItemString(an_CurrentRow,is_NameColumn)
io_TreeItem.Data = la_CodeValue
ln_ItemHandle = ao_TreeObject.InsertItemLast(ist_NodeInfo[ln_ParentRow].ItemHandle,io_TreeItem)
ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].FilledFlag = True
ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].ItemHandle = ln_ItemHandle
ist_NodeInfo[an_CurrentRow].DataValue = la_CodeValue
return
end subroutine

on u_levelcodeclass.create
TriggerEvent( this, "constructor" )
end on

on u_levelcodeclass.destroy
TriggerEvent( this, "destructor" )
end on

event constructor;call super::constructor;is_ClassName = "LevelCodeClass"
return
end event

public functi
on boolean logout ();if ib_LogOn then
 	Delete From SS_REGISTER
	Where XTSB = :in_SystemID AND YHDM = :is_UserID AND WKDZ = :is_CardAddress
	Using io_Environment ;
	commit using io_Environment ;	
	ib_LogOn = False
end if
return True
end function

public subroutine password ();io_Parm.LongParm[1] = in_SystemID
io_Parm.StringParm[1] = is_UserID
OpenWithParm(w_Password,io_Parm)
return
end subroutine

public function date getdate ();return Date(id_SysDateTime)
end function

public function datetime systemdate ();
id_SysDateTime = gf_server_date()

return id_SysDateTime
end function

public function string getuserid ();return is_UserID
end function

public function string getusername ();return is_UserName
end function

end function

public function string getsystemname ();return is_SystemName
end function

public function integer getsystemid ();return in_SystemID
end function

public function datetime getdatetime ();id_SysDateTime = SystemDate()
return id_SysDateTime
end function

public function boolean settransobject (ref transaction a_transaction);io_Environment = a_transaction
if not IsValid(io_Environment) then return False
return True
end function

public function boolean login (integer an_systemid);Long ln_Counter

/*********************************************/
/*进行更新检测,如果有更新则进行升级          */
/********************************************/

//if gf_update_file(io_environment,an_systemid,is_inifilename) <> 0 then
//	return false
//end if

id_SysDateTime = SystemDate()
Select Count(*) Into :ln_Counter
From SS_OPERATE_USER
Where XTSB = :an_SystemID And YHDM = 'ADMIN'
Using io_Environment;
if ln_Counter<=0 then
	Insert Into SS_OPERATE_USER (XTSB,YHDM,YHMC,YHKL,QYBZ,YHQX) 
	Values (:an_SystemID,'ADMIN','管理员','',1,'Admi')
	Using io_Environment; 
	commit Using io_Environment ;
end if
// 提示用户登录
io_Parm.TransParm = io_Environment
io_Parm.LongParm[1] = an_SystemID
if is_commandline > '' then
	
	integer li_pos1,li_pos2,ll_cpu
	string ls_userpass
	li_pos1 = pos(is_commandline,'[')
	li_pos2 = pos(is_commandline,']')
			
	in_SystemID = an_SystemID
	is_UserID = mid(is_commandline,li_pos1 + 1,li_pos2 - li_pos1  - 1)

	li_pos1 = pos(is_commandline,'[',li_pos2)
	li_pos2 = pos(is_commandline,']',li_pos2 + 1)
	
	ls_userpass = mid(is_commandline,li_pos1 + 1,li_pos2 - li_pos1  - 1)
   
	li_pos1 = pos(is_commandline,'[',li_pos2)
	li_pos2 = pos(is_commandline,']',li_pos2 + 1)
	
		
	ll_cpu = long(mid(is_commandline,li_pos1 + 1,li_pos2 - li_pos1  - 1))
	if cpu() - ll_cpu > 120000 then
		messagebox('提示','登录时间过长,请重试!')
		return false		
	end if
	
	SELECT YHKL INTO :is_UserPass From SS_OPERATE_USER
	Where XTSB = :an_SystemID And YHDM = :is_UserID using io_Environment ;
	if io_Environment.sqlcode <> 0 then 
		messagebox('提示','登录口令不符,请检查设置!')
		return false
	end if
	
	if isnull(ls_userpass) then ls_userpass = ''
	
	if is_UserPass <> ls_userpass then
		messagebox('提示','登录口令不符,请检查设置!')
		return false
	end if
	
else
	OpenWithParm(w_Login,io_Parm)
	io_Parm = Message.PowerObjectParm
	if not isvalid(io_Parm) then return false
	if io_Parm.LongParm[1]<>1 then return False
	// 登录成功
	in_SystemID = an_SystemID
	is_UserID = io_Parm.StringParm[1]
	is_UserPass = io_Parm.StringParm[2]
end if
// 设置系统名称、系统版本
Integer	li_xtbb

Select XTMC, XTBB Into :is_SystemName , :li_xtbb
	From SS_INSTALL
	Where XTSB = :in_SystemID
	Using io_Environment;

is_SystemVer = String(li_xtbb / 1000)	

//取医院名称
Select CSZ Into :is_HospitalName
	From GY_XTCS Where CSMC ='HOSPITAL' Using io_Environment;			
Select CSZ Into :is_HospitalName1
	From GY_XTCS Where CSMC ='HOSPITAL1' Using io_Environment;	
Select CSZ Into :is_HospitalName2
	From GY_XTCS Where CSMC ='HOSPITAL2' Using io_Environment;	
	
// 设置系统用户名称
if in_SystemID = 9999 then 
	Select YHMC,YHQX,YHZM Into :is_UserName,:is_rightname,:is_groupname
		From SS_OPERATE_USER
		Where YHDM = :is_UserID
		Using io_Environment;
else
	Select YHMC,YHQX,YHZM Into :is_UserName,:is_rightname,:is_groupname
		From SS_OPERATE_USER
		Where XTSB = :in_SystemID And YHDM = :is_UserID
		Using io_Environment;
	end if
//取网卡地址

SELECT QXJB INTO :ii_qxjb FROM SS_GROUP 
 WHERE XTSB = :in_SystemID AND YHZM = :is_groupname;
if is_UserID = 'ADMIN'  then
	ii_qxjb = 2
end 
if

base_info.syscode = an_systemid   //由于在gf_ProfileString中需要用到系统识别,因此先赋值.

//取帮助文件名称
is_HelpFileName = Trim(ProfileString(is_IniFileName,"Option","HelpFileName_" + string(an_systemid),""))

commit Using io_Environment ;

ib_LogOn = True
return True



end function

public subroutine set_menu_right (ref menu ao_menu);// 设置菜单权限
DataStore lo_CDQX
 
if Upper(is_UserID)="ADMIN" then //如果为系统管理员,则不用设置菜单权限,并且会导入菜单项目信息
	get_menu_name(ao_menu) 
	return
end if
lo_CDQX = Create DataStore
lo_CDQX.DataObject = 'd_CDQX'  //菜单权限
lo_CDQX.SetTransObject(io_Environment)
Select YHZM Into :is_GroupName
	From SS_OPERATE_USER
	Where XTSB = :in_SystemID And YHDM = :is_UserID
	Using io_Environment;
if (not(IsNull(is_GroupName)))and(io_Environment.SQLCode<>-1) then
	IF	gb_EAServer THEN
		blob lblb_data
		iuo_EAS_system.retrieve_cdqx(in_SystemID,is_GroupName,lblb_data)

		lo_CDQX.setfullstate(lblb_data)
	else
		lo_CDQX.Retrieve(in_SystemID,is_GroupName)
	end if
	
	il_MenuLevel = 0  // 菜单层次置0
	Set_Menu_Right_Loop(ao_Menu,lo_CDQX)
end if
Destroy lo_CDQX
return

end subroutine

private subroutine set_menu_right_loop (ref menu ao_menu, ref datastore ao_datastore);//设置菜单权限的递归循环
long ln_Length,ln_Circle,ln_FindRow,ln_TotalRows
string ls_MenuItemName
ln_Length = UpperBound(ao_Menu.Item[])
if ln_length>0 then
	ln_TotalRows = ao_DataStore.RowCount()
	for ln_Circle=1 to ln_Length
		
		il_MenuLevel ++  // 菜单层次加一
		
		ls_MenuItemName = ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle].ClassName()
		IF ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle].Visible THEN
			if ls_MenuItemName<>'-' then
				
				IF ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle].Tag <> "AlwaysEnabled" THEN // 永远可以使用
					IF il_MenuLevel > 1 THEN  // 第一层菜单不控制权限
						ln_FindRow = ao_DataStore.Find("CDQX='"+ls_MenuItemName+"'",1,ln_TotalRows)
						if ln_FindRow<1 then
							ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle].Enabled = False
						else
							ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle].Enabled = True
						end if
					END IF
				END IF
				Set_Menu_Right_Loop(ao_Menu.Item[ln_Circle],ao_DataStore)
			end if
		END IF
		
		il_MenuLevel --  // 菜单层次减一
		
	next
end if
return
end subroutine

public subroutine browselog ();// 浏览系统信息

io_Parm.TransParm = io_Environment
io_Parm.LongParm[1] = in_SystemID
OpenWithParm(w_Log_Info,io_Parm)
return

end subroutine

public subroutine browsesysinfo ();// 浏览系统信息

io_parm.TransParm = io_Environment
io_parm.LongParm[1] = in_SystemID
//OpenWithParm(w_Sys_Info,io_Parm)
return
end subroutine

public function boolean checkversion (integer an_versionlow, integer an_versionhigh);Long ln_Version
Select XTBB Into :ln_Version From SS_INSTALL
Where XTSB = :in_SystemID
Using io_Environment;
if (ln_Version<an_VersionLow)or(ln_Version>an_VersionHigh) then return False
return True

end function

public function boolean checkdependence ();long ln_Counter
Select Count(*) Into :ln_Counter
From SS_RELATION
Where XTSB = :in_SystemID
	And YLLB Not In (Select XTLB From SS_INSTALL Where XTAZ = 'Y' And CSHF = 'Y')
Using io_Environment;
if ln_Counter>0 then return False
return True


end function

public subroutine setmenuright (ref menu ao_menu);// 设置菜单权限
DataStore lo_CDQX
String ls_GroupName
lo_CDQX = Create DataStore
lo_CDQX.DataObject = 'd_CDQX'  //菜单权限
lo_CDQX.SetTransObject(io_Environment)
Select YHZM Into :ls_GroupName
	From SS_Operate_User
	Where XTSB = :in_SystemID And YHDM = :is_UserID
	Using io_Environment;
if (not(IsNull(ls_GroupName)))and(io_Environment.SQLCode<>-1) then
	IF	gb_EAServer THEN
		blob lblb_data
		iuo_EAS_system.retrieve_cdqx(in_SystemID,ls_GroupName,lblb_data)

		lo_CDQX.setfullstate(lblb_data)
	else
		lo_CDQX.Retrieve(in_SystemID,ls_GroupName)
	end if
	il_MenuLevel = 0  // 菜单层次置0
	Set_Menu_Right_Loop(ao_Menu,lo_CDQX)
end if
Destroy lo_CDQX
return

end subroutine

public function boolean hadrunning ();u_SingleInstanceClass u_Single
if u_Single.HadRunning()>1 then 
	return True
end if
return False
end function

end subroutine

public subroutine right_manager (ref menu ao_menu);//用户权限管理
io_Parm.TransParm = io_Environment
io_Parm.LongParm[1] = in_SystemID
io_Parm.MenuParm = ao_Menu
OpenWithParm(w_Right_Manager,io_Parm)
return

end subroutine

public function string getsystemver ();return is_SystemVer
end function

public function integer get_menu_name (ref menu ao_menu);//遍历菜单，返回所有的菜单名称，并保存到表WH_MENU中
datastore lds_menu
int i,li_return 
lds_menu = create datastore
lds_menu.dataobject = "d_cdmc_get"
lds_menu.settransobject(io_environment)


li_return = lds_menu.retrieve(in_SystemID) //检索出所有本系统的菜单项目
if li_return = -1 then goto errorhandle

for i = lds_menu.rowcount() to 1 step -1 //首先删除所有的菜单项目
	lds_menu.deleterow(i)
next
il_menulevel_get = 0
get_menu_name_loop(ao_menu,lds_menu)//

io_transaction.uf_begin_transaction(sqlca)
if lds_menu.update() = -1 then
	io_transaction.uf_commit_transaction(sqlca)
	goto errorhandle
else
	io_transaction.uf_rollback_transaction(sqlca)
	destroy lds_menu
	return 1
end if

errorhandle:
destroy lds_menu
return -1

end function

public subroutine get_menu_name_loop (ref menu au_menu, ref datastore ads_menu);//递归执行函数，得到每个菜单项目的名称
long ln_Length,ln_Circle,ln_FindRow,ln_TotalRows
string ls_MenuItemName,ls_MenuItemName_Parent
long ll_insert
string ls_MenuItemText
long ll_pos
long ll_index = 1
ln_Length = UpperBound(au_menu.Item[])

if ln_length>0 then
	ln_TotalRows = ads_menu.RowCount()
	for ln_Circle=1 to ln_Length
		
		il_MenuLevel_Get ++  // 菜单层次加一
		
		ls_MenuItemName_Parent = au_menu.ClassName()
		ls_MenuItemName = au_menu.Item[ln_Circle].ClassName()
		ls_MenuItemText = au_menu.Item[ln_Circle].text
		IF au_menu.Item[ln_Circle].Visible THEN
			if ls_MenuItemText<>'-' then
				IF au_menu.Item[ln_Circle].Tag <> "AlwaysEnabled" THEN // 永远可以使用
						ll_insert = ads_menu.insertrow(0)
						ads_menu.object.xtsb[ll_insert] = in_systemid //系统识别
						ads_menu.object.cdmc[ll_insert] = ls_MenuItemName //菜单名称
						ads_menu.object.sjmc[ll_insert] = ls_MenuItemName_Parent //上级菜单名称
						ads_menu.object.cdcc[ll_insert] = il_MenuLevel_Get //菜单层次
						ads_menu.object.cdsx[ll_insert] = ll_index //菜单顺序
						ll_index = ll_index + 1
						
						ll_Pos = Pos(ls_MenuItemText,"&")
						IF ll_Pos > 0 THEN
						ll_Pos = Pos(ls_MenuItemText,"(")
							IF ll_Pos > 0 THEN
								ls_MenuItemText = Left(ls_MenuItemText,ll_Pos - 1)  // 菜单具有后缀 (&A)
							ELSE
								ls_MenuItemText = Mid (ls_MenuItemText,4)           // 菜单具有前缀 &1.
							END IF	
						END IF
						
						ads_menu.object.cdwb[ll_insert] = ls_MenuItemText  //菜单项目文本
						
				END IF
				get_menu_name_loop(au_menu.Item[ln_Circle],ads_menu)
			end if
		END IF
		
		il_MenuLevel_Get --  // 菜单层次减一
		
	next
end if
return
end subroutine

public function boolean writelog (string as_modulename, string as_infotext);Insert Into SS_LOG(XTSB,JLSJ,YHBH,MKMC,JLWB,WKDZ)
	Values (:in_SystemID,:id_SysDatetime,:is_UserID,:as_moduleName, 	:as_InfoText,:is_CardAddress)
	Using io_Environment;
	
if io_Environment.SQLCode<>0 then
	rollback Using io_Environment ;
	return False	
end if

commit Using io_Environment ;
return True
end function

public subroutine uf_getsytle ();string ls_style


ls_style = gf_getpara( base_info.syscode,'STYLE', 'default', '显示风格')

datastore lds_temp
lds_temp = create datastore
lds_temp.dataobject = 'd_style'
lds_temp.settransobject(sqlca)
lds_temp.retrieve(ls_style)
if lds_temp.rowcount() = 1 then

	base_info.style = ls_style
	base_info.stylecolor.object =  lds_temp.object.object[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.datawindow_line = lds_temp.object.datawindow_line[1]
  
	base_info.stylecolor.datawindow = lds_temp.object.datawindow1[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.window =  lds_temp.object.window[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.splitbar	= lds_temp.object.splitbar[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.shortcut	= lds_temp.object.shortcut[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.tree	= lds_temp.object.tree[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.progress	= lds_temp.object.progress[1]
	base_info.stylecolor.menu	= lds_temp.object.menu[1]
	
end if


destroy lds_temp
end subroutine

public function boolean initialize (transaction ao_environment, string as_dwname, boolean ab_refresh);Integer ln_Return
SetEnvironment(ao_Environment)
ln_Return = SetObject(as_DWName)  //数据窗口
if ln_Return<>1 then return False
if ab_Refresh then 
	if Refresh()<1 then return False
end if
return True
end function

type variables

transaction trans_working

end variables

on w_log_info.create
this.cb_activate_default=create cb_activate_default
this.cb_1=create cb_1
this.uo_1=create uo_1

this.cb_detail}

on w_log_info.destroy
destroy(this.cb_activate_default)
destroy(this.cb_1)

end on

event open;io_parm = message.PowerObjectParm
trans_working = io_parm.TransParm
gf_center_window(this)
// 取得需要操作的系统的信息
string   ls_system_name
integer li_system_id
long ll_parts[2]
ll_parts[1]=600
ll_parts[2]=-1
uo_1.of_setparts(ll_parts)
uo_1.of_settext(0,0,'双击左键查询事件详情!单击表头按升序排列，双击表头按降序排列!')

//取得需要操作的系统的信息
li_system_id = io_parm.LongParm[1]
if li_system_id <= 0 then		// all system manager
	ddlb_system_list.additem('---' + ' - ' + '全部系统')
	DECLARE CUR_SYSTEM_LIST CURSOR FOR
	SELECT XTSB, XTMC 
	FROM SS_INSTALL
	USING trans_working;
	OPEN CUR_SYSTEM_LIST;
	FETCH CUR_SYSTEM_LIST into :li_system_id, :ls_system_name;
	do while io_parm.TransParm.SqlCode = 0
		ddlb_system_list.additem(string(li_system_id, '000') + ' - ' + ls_system_name)
		FETCH CUR_SYSTEM_LIST into :li_system_id, :ls_system_name;
	loop
	CLOSE CUR_SYSTEM_LIST;
	this.title = "系统日志 - " + '全部系统'
else	// one system manager
	SELECT XTMC into :ls_system_name
	FROM SS_INSTALL
	WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id
	USING trans_working;

	ddlb_system_list.additem(string(li_system_id, '000') + ' - ' + ls_system_name)
	this.title = "系统日志 - " + ls_system_name
end if

// 设置各数据窗口
dw_log_list.settransobject(trans_working)

ddlb_system_list.selectitem(1)
ddlb_system_list.triggerevent(selectionchanged!)



end event

if ls_system_id = '---' then
	dw_log_list.setfilter("")
else
	dw_log_list.setfilter("ss_log_xtsb = " + ls_system_id)
end if
dw_log_list.retrieve()

	dw_list.SelectRow ( ll_find, true ) 
	dw_detail.retrieve(ls_style)

		Messagebox('提示','设置成功!')
	end if

global type w_password from w_window

integer y = 360

global w_password w_password

type variables
n_exchange io_Parm

String                is_UserID
String                is_UserName
String                is_UserPass
Boolean            ib_UserExist        // 是否输入用户
integer              ii_qybz 
datetime idt_klxgrq
end variables

on w_password.create
int iCurrent
call super::create
this.pb_2=create pb_2
this.pb_confirm=create pb_confirm
this.dw_1=create dw_1
this.gb_title=create gb_title
iCurrent=UpperBound(this.Control)
this.Control[iCurrent+1]=this.pb_2
this.Control[iCurrent+2]=this.pb_confirm
this.Control[iCurrent+3]=this.dw_1
this.Control[iCurrent+4]=this.gb_title
end on

on w_password.destroy
call super::destroy
destroy(this.pb_2)
destroy(this.pb_confirm)
destroy(this.dw_1)
destroy(this.gb_title)
end on

event open;call super::open;//// 输入参数:
//// io_parm.LongParm[1]	系统识别
//// io_parm.StringParm[1]	用户代码
//gf_Center_Window(This)


string ls_group_name
io_Parm = Message.PowerObjectParm

SELECT YHZM
INTO  :ls_group_name
FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
WHERE XTSB = :io_parm.LongParm[1] AND YHDM = :io_parm.StringParm[1]
USING io_parm.transParm;

if isnull(ls_group_name) then ls_group_name = "***"

gb_title.text = string(io_parm.LongParm[1]) + ' : ' + ls_group_name
gf_center_window(This)



pb_confirm.Enabled = False

dw_1.InsertRow(0)

end event

event key;GraphicObject lgo_object

IF Key = KeyEnter! THEN
	lgo_object = GetFocus()
	IF TypeOf(lgo_object) = CommandButton! THEN
		lgo_object.TriggerEvent("Clicked")
	END IF	
ELSEIF key = KeyScrollLock! THEN 
	u_SingleInstanceClass luo_exe
	string ls_exename,ls_curname,ls_key
	u_api_function  luo_api
	if Handle(GetApplication()) =0 then return  //如果在开发模式，则立即返回
	luo_api.uf_system_maskreset(false)   //解ctrl+Alt+del 
	ls_key = "HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce"
	ls_exename = luo_exe.get_exename()  //取得当前应用程序文件名（全路径）
	RegistryGet(ls_key,ls_exename,ls_curname)  //取得当前注册表中的应用程序名
	if ls_curname = ls_exename then   
		RegistryDelete (ls_key,ls_exename)  //注册应用程序名
	end if
END IF	



end event

end type

type pb_confirm from vu_buttonok within w_password
integer x = 192
integer y = 864
integer taborder = 40
end type

event clicked;call super::clicked;
string ls_password1,ls_password2
string ls_yhdm,ls_error,ls_yhkl_old
Int li_ret,li_mmjyqy
datetime ldt_klxgrq
dw_1.accepttext()
ls_yhdm=dw_1.object.yhdm[1]
ls_password1=dw_1.object.password1[1]
ls_password2=dw_1.object.password2[1]
IF isnull(ls_password1) then ls_password1=''
IF isnull(ls_password2) then ls_password2=''

if ls_password1<>ls_password2 then
	messagebox('提示!','新密码和确认密码不统一,请重新修改!')
	return 
end if

integer li_syscode
li_syscode = base_info.syscode
integer li_klty



//密码控制  ljp 2015-10-30 begin
li_mmjyqy = Long(gf_getpara( 0, 'MMJYQY', '0', '密码校验启用'))
IF li_mmjyqy > 0 THEN
	
	//判断新密码和老密码不能一样
	ls_yhkl_old = dw_1.Object.yhkl[1]
	if ls_yhkl_old=ls_password1 then
		dw_1.Object.password1[1] = ''
		dw_1.Object.password2[1] = ''
		dw_1.SetFocus( )
		dw_1.SetColumn("password1")
		MessageBox('提示!','新密码和老密码不能一样,请重新修改!')
		RETURN
	end if
	setnull(ldt_klxgrq)
	li_ret = gf_check_password(ls_password1,ls_yhdm,ldt_klxgrq,ls_error)
ELSE
	li_ret = 1
END IF

IF li_ret < 0 THEN
	/* 密码修改 */
	dw_1.Object.password1[1] = ''
	dw_1.Object.password2[1] = ''
	dw_1.SetFocus( )
	dw_1.SetColumn("password1")
	
	MessageBox('提示',ls_error)
	RETURN
END IF
//密码控制  ljp 2015-10-30 begin



li_klty = integer(gf_getpara(0, 'KLTY', '1','密码统一'))
ls_password1 = gu_char.uf_encode(ls_password1)


if li_klty = 1 then
	//密码统一
	UPDATE SS_OPERATE_USER
	SET YHKL = :ls_password1
	WHERE  YHDM = :ls_yhdm 
	USING io_parm.TransParm;
else
	
	UPDATE SS_OPERATE_USER
	SET YHKL = :ls_password1
	WHERE  XTSB = :li_syscode AND 
			 YHDM = :ls_yhdm
	USING io_parm.TransParm;
end if
if io_parm.TransParm.sqlcode = 0 then 
	COMMIT USING io_parm.TransParm;
	messagebox('提示','修改密码成功,请牢记该密码!')
else
	Rollback USING io_parm.TransParm;
	messagebox('提示','修改密码失败，请重试!')
	return
end if


closewithreturn(parent,'1')
end event

//dw_1::editchanged()
//***************************************************
IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHDM" THEN
	IF ib_UserExist THEN
		ib_UserExist = False
		This.Object.yhmc[Row] = ""
		This.Object.yhkl[Row] = ""
		pb_confirm.Enabled = False
	END IF	
END IF

end event

event itemchanged;IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHDM" THEN	
	ib_UserExist = False	
	IF IsNull(Data) THEN Data = ""
	IF Trim(Data) = "" THEN RETURN	
	integer li_syscode
	li_syscode = base_info.syscode
	SELECT SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM,
			 SS_OPERATE_USER.YHMC,
			 SS_OPERATE_USER.YHKL,
			 SS_OPERATE_USER.KLXGRQ
	  INTO :is_UserID,
			 :is_UserName,
			 :is_UserPass,
			 :idt_klxgrq
	  FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
	 WHERE SS_OPERATE_USER.XTSB = :li_syscode AND 
	 		 SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM = :Data 
	 USING io_Parm.TransParm ;	
	IF io_Parm.TransParm.sqlcode = 0 THEN	
		
		/* 判断用户代码大小写是否完全匹配 */
		IF is_UserID <> Data THEN
			This.SelectText(1,10)
			RETURN 0
		END IF			
		ib_UserExist = True		
		is_UserPass = Trim(is_UserPass)
		IF IsNull(is_UserPass) THEN is_UserPass = ""		
		dw_1.Object.yhmc[Row] = is_UserName
		
	ELSE
		This.SelectText(1,10)
		RETURN
	END IF	
END IF

end event

event itemerror;return 1
end event

IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHKL" THEN
	IF NOT ib_UserExist THEN 
		This.SetColumn("YHDM")
		return
	end if
	
	integer li_syscode
	li_syscode = base_info.syscode
	
	SELECT XTSB,YHZM
	INTO  :ll_xtsb,:ls_group_name
	FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
	WHERE  XTSB =:li_syscode and YHDM = :ls_yhdm
	USING io_parm.transParm;
	
	if isnull(ls_group_name) then ls_group_name = "***"
	
	gb_title.text = string(ll_xtsb) + ' : ' + ls_group_name

END IF


IF UPPER(dwo.name)='PASSWORD1' thenype

global function string gf_decode_new (string as_input, string as_pzwj);//原字符串倒置：
//
//取KEYCODE.DLL文件（取本地，取网络之分)
//
//每个字符增加 ASC码，值为KEYCODE.dll中的第Ｎ + m（原字符长度） 位（跟当前符符有关）的个位数，
//如取值不到，直接为当前位置数的个位



Integer i,li_add
String ls_return = '', ls_char,ls_key
String ls_keycode,ls_key_path,ls_keycode_temp,ls_error
Boolean lbl_ret

IF as_pzwj <> '0' THEN
	lbl_ret = gf_get_keycode(ls_keycode_temp,ls_error)
	if lbl_ret=false then
		gf_applog(" "+ls_error)
	end if
END IF

IF IsNull(ls_keycode_temp) Or ls_keycode_temp = '' THEN
	if as_pzwj<>'0' then
		HALT CLOSE
		return ''
	else
		ls_key_path = "KEYCODE.dll"
		ls_keycode = ProfileString (ls_key_path,'KEY',"KEY","ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ")
	end if
//	IF as_pzwj = '1' THEN
//		IF FileExists("\\fey02.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL") THEN
//			ls_key_path = "\\fey02.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//		ELSE
//			ls_key_path = "\\fey01.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//		END IF
//	ELSEIF as_pzwj = '2' THEN
//		ls_key_path = "\\173.1.0.1\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//	ELSEIF as_pzwj = '3' THEN
//		ls_key_path = "\\168.168.1.4\newhis\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//	ELSE
//		ls_key_path = "KEYCODE.dll"
//	END IF
//	ls_keycode = ProfileString (ls_key_path,'KEY',"KEY","ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ")
	//lbl_ret=gf_get_keycode(ls_keycode,ls_error)
ELSE
	ls_keycode = ls_keycode_temp
END IF



//ls_keycode = ProfileString (ls_key_path,'KEY',"KEY","ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ")

as_input = Mid(as_input,1,Len(as_input) -1 )
FOR i = 1 To Len(as_input)
	ls_char = Mid(as_input, Len(as_input) + 1 - i, 1)
	ls_key  = Mid(ls_keycode, Len(as_input) + (Len(as_input) + 1 - i) , 1)
	
	IF IsNull(ls_key) Or ls_key = '' THEN
		li_add =  Mod(Len(as_input) + 1 - i,10)
	ELSE
		li_add =  Mod(Asc(ls_key)ao_treeobject as_rootname createtree as_parentname
setparentcolumn
as_codevalue
ab_leafonly
as_childarray
an_codevalue
an_childarray
getrootcode
ab_canself
childrecursiondecimal
childrecursionstring
an_currentrow
createrecursion
+constructor
BCtreeview.S
QCtreeview.L

u_levelcodeclass
transaction

global type w_user_manager_import_user from window
integer x = 1056

global w_user_manager_import_user w_user_manager_import_user

type variables
n_exchange io_Parm

Long            il_OldRow
end variables

forward prototypes
public subroutine wf_addks ()
end prototypes

public subroutine wf_addks ();DataStore	lds_ksdm
Long		ll_Row
Long		ll_RowCount
String	ls_Code , ls_Name , ls_pydm

lds_ksdm = Create DataStore
lds_ksdm.DataObject = "dd_ksdm_all_down"
lds_ksdm.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm)
lds_ksdm.Retrieve()

ll_RowCount = lds_ksdm.RowCount()

IF ddlb_ks.TotalItems() > 0 THEN ddlb_ks.Reset()

ddlb_ks.AddItem("0","所有科室","")

FOR ll_Row = 1 TO ll_RowCount
	ls_Code = string(lds_ksdm.Object.ksdm[ll_Row])
	ls_Name = lds_ksdm.Object.ksmc[ll_Row]
	ls_pydm = lds_ksdm.Object.pydm[ll_Row]
	ddlb_ks.AddItem(ls_Code,ls_Name,ls_pydm)
NEXT

ddlb_ks.ItemSelect(1)

end subroutine

on w_user_manager_import_user.create
this.dw_2=create dw_2
this.uo_statbar=create uo_statbar
this.st_1=create st_1
this.cb_exit=create cb_exit
this.cb_import=create cb_import
this.ddlb_ks=create ddlb_ks
this.gb_1=create gb_1
this.dw_1=create dw_1
this.Control[]={this.dw_2,&
this.uo_statbar,&
this.st_1,&
this.cb_exit,&
this.cb_import,&
this.ddlb_ks,&
this.gb_1,&
this.dw_1}
end on

on w_user_manager_import_user.destroy
destroy(this.dw_2)
destroy(this.uo_statbar)
destroy(this.st_1)
destroy(this.cb_exit)
dest

global function string gf_encode_new (string as_input, string as_pzwj);//原字符串倒置：
//
//取KEYCODE.DLL文件（取本地，取网络之分)
//
//每个字符增加 ASC码，值为KEYCODE.dll中的第Ｎ + m（原字符长度） 位（跟当前符符有关）的个位数，如取值不到，直接为当前位置数的个位
//最后增加字符串位数的个位数,使之长度＋１，看起来不一

String ls_return = '', ls_char,ls_key
String ls_keycode,ls_key_path,ls_keycode_temp,ls_error

IF as_pzwj <> '0' THEN
	lbl_ret = gf_get_keycode(ls_keycode_temp,ls_error)
	if lbl_ret=false then
		gf_applog(" "+ls_error)
	end if
END IF
IF IsNull(ls_keycode_temp) Or ls_keycode_temp = '' THEN
	if as_pzwj<>'0' then
		HALT CLOSE
		return ''
	else
		ls_key_path = "KEYCODE.dll"
		ls_keycode = ProfileString (ls_key_path,'KEY',"KEY","ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ")
	end if
//	IF as_pzwj = '1' THEN
//		IF FileExists("\\fey02.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL") THEN
//			ls_key_path = "\\fey02.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//		ELSE
//			ls_key_path = "\\fey01.fey.com\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//		END IF
//	ELSEIF as_pzwj = '2' THEN
//		ls_key_path = "\\173.1.0.1\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//	ELSEIF as_pzwj = '3' THEN
//		ls_key_path = "\\168.168.1.4\newhis\config\KEYCODE.DLL"
//	ELSE
//		ls_key_path = "KEYCODE.dll"
//	END IF

global type gf_get_gyini from function_object

global function string gf_getygxm (string as_ygdm);//*******************************************************************************//
// Function  : 取得用户名称
// Memo      :系统标志=0,表示忽略系统标志
//*******************************************************************************//

string ls_name

select YGXM into :ls_name from GY_YGDM  WHERE YGDM =:as_ygdm;

if isnull(ls_name) then ls_name=""

return ls_name
end function

type cb_import from commandbutton within w_user_manager_import_user

event clicked;Long   	ll_Row
Long   	ll_RowCount
Long		ll_Temp
string   ls_yhkl,ls_yhdm
dw_2.Reset()

ll_RowCount = dw_1.RowCount()

FOR ll_Row = 1 TO ll_RowCount
	IF dw_1.IsSelected(ll_Row) THEN
		ll_Temp = dw_2.InsertRow(0)
		dw_2.Object.xtsb[ll_Temp] = io_parm.LongParm[1]
		dw_2.Object.yhzm[ll_Temp] = io_parm.StringParm[1]
		dw_2.Object.yhdm[ll_Temp] = dw_1.Object.ygdm[ll_Row]
		dw_2.Object.yhmc[ll_Temp] = dw_1.Object.ygxm[ll_Row]
		ls_yhdm = dw_1.Object.ygdm[ll_Row]
		ls_yhkl = ''
		SELECT max(YHKL) INTO :ls_yhkl FROM SS_OPERATE_USER  WHERE  YHDM = :ls_yhdm;
		dw_2.Object.yhkl[ll_Temp] = ls_yhkl
		dw_2.Object.yhjb[ll_Temp] = 1
	END IF	
NEXT

gu_transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(io_parm.transParm)

IF dw_2.Update() <> 1 THEN
	gu_transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(io_parm.transParm)
	MessageBox("提示信息","数据保存失败，请检查!",Exclamation!)
	RETURN
END IF

gu_transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(io_parm.transParm)

FOR ll_Row = ll_RowCount TO 1 STEP -1
	IF dw_1.IsSelected(ll_Row) THEN dw_1.DeleteRow(ll_Row)
NEXT

end event

type variables
// input parameter
n_exchange io_Parm
transaction trans_working

integer ii_SystemID
String   is_GroupCode

Integer    ii_MenuLevel  // 菜单层次
Boolean  ib_RightChanged
end variables

forward prototypes
public subroutine wf_createmenutv (menu am_menu, long al_handle)
public subroutine wf_refreshright (long al_handle)
public subroutine wf_setchildright (long al_handle, boolean ab_haveright)
public subroutine wf_setparentright (long al_handle)
