public subroutine wf_createmenutv (menu am_menu, long al_handle);//*************************************************************************************//
// Function  : 生成菜单层次树
//
// Parameter : am_Menu
//					al_Handle
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//*************************************************************************************//
Long   ll_Pos
Long   ll_Handle
Long   ll_MenuItem
Long   ll_TotalItems
Long   ll_MenuItemNumber
String ls_MenuItemName
String ls_MenuItemText
String ls_MenuItemPicture

TreeViewItem ltvi_Item

ll_TotalItems = UpperBound(am_Menu.Item[])

IF ll_TotalItems = 0 THEN RETURN

FOR ll_MenuItem = 1 TO ll_TotalItems
	ii_MenuLevel ++
	
	ls_MenuItemName = am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].ClassName()
	ls_MenuItemText = am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].Text
	//ls_MenuItemPicture = am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].ToolbarItemName
	
	ll_Pos = Pos(ls_MenuItemText,"&")
	
	IF ll_Pos > 0 THEN
		ll_Pos = Pos(ls_MenuItemText,"(")
		IF ll_Pos > 0 THEN
			ls_MenuItemText = Left(ls_MenuItemText,ll_Pos - 1)  // 菜单具有后缀 (&A)
		ELSE
			ls_MenuItemText = Mid (ls_MenuItemText,4)           // 菜单具有前缀 &1.
		END IF	
	END IF
	
	IF NOT am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].Visible THEN GOTO Process_Next
	IF ls_MenuItemText = "-" 				 THEN GOTO Process_Next
	
	IF am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].Tag = "AlwaysEnabled" THEN GOTO Process_Next
	
	ll_MenuItemNumber ++
	
	ltvi_Item.Data  = ls_MenuItemName
	ltvi_Item.Label = String(ll_MenuItemNumber) + "." + left((ls_MenuItemText+space(20)),20) + left((am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].Tag+space(20)),20) + left((am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem].ToolbarItemName+space(20)),20)
	
	IF ii_MenuLevel = 1 THEN
		ltvi_Item.PictureIndex 			 = 1
		ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = 1
	ELSE
		ltvi_Item.PictureIndex         = 3
		ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = 3
	END 
IF
	
//	ltvi_Item.StatePictureName = 	ls_MenuItemPicture
	ll_Handle = tv_1.InsertItemLast(al_Handle,ltvi_Item)
	
	wf_CreateMenuTV(am_Menu.Item[ll_MenuItem],ll_Handle)
	
	Process_Next:
	ii_MenuLevel --
NEXT

end subroutine

public subroutine wf_refreshright (long al_handle);//***********************************************************************************//
// Function  : 刷新用户组权限
//
// Parameter : al_Handle
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//***********************************************************************************//

TreeViewItem ltvi_Item
Long         ll_Handle
Long         ll_Row
String       ls_MenuName
Integer      li_PictureIndex

IF al_Handle = 0 THEN	// 父节点为根节点
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(RootTreeItem!,0)
ELSE
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(ChildTreeItem!,al_Handle)
END IF

IF ll_Handle = -1 THEN RETURN

DO WHILE ll_Handle <> -1 
	tv_1.GetItem(ll_Handle, ltvi_Item)
	
	ls_MenuName = ltvi_Item.Data
	
	ll_Row = dw_cdqx.Find("cdqx = '" + ls_MenuName + "'",1,dw_cdqx.RowCount())
	
	IF ll_Row > 0 THEN
		IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN
			li_PictureIndex = 1
		ELSE
			li_PictureIndex = 3
		END IF	
	ELSE
		IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN
			li_PictureIndex = 2
		ELSE
			li_PictureIndex = 4
		END IF	
	END IF
	
	ltvi_Item.PictureIndex 			 = li_PictureIndex
	ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = li_PictureIndex
	
	tv_1.SetItem(ll_Handle, ltvi_Item)
	
	wf_RefreshRight(ll_Handle)
	
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(NextTreeItem!,ll_Handle)
LOOP

end subroutine

public subroutine wf_setchildright (long al_handle, boolean ab_haveright);//***********************************************************************************//
// Function  : 设置子项权限
//
// Parameter : al_Handle
//             ab_HaveRight
//
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//***********************************************************************************//

TreeViewItem ltvi_Item
Long         ll_Handle
Integer      li_PictureIndex

IF al_Handle = 0 THEN	// 父节点为根节点
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(RootTreeItem!,0)
ELSE
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(ChildTreeItem!,al_Handle)
END IF

IF ll_Handle = -1 THEN RETURN

DO WHILE ll_Handle <> -1 
	tv_1.GetItem(ll_Handle,ltvi_Item)
	
	IF ab_HaveRight THEN  // 设置成有权限
		IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN  // 第一层
			li_PictureIndex = 1
		ELSE
			li_PictureIndex = 3
		END IF	
	ELSE
		IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN  // 第一层
			li_PictureIndex = 2
		ELSE
			li_PictureIndex = 4
		END IF	
	END IF	
	
	ltvi_Item.PictureIndex         = li_PictureIndex
	ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = li_PictureIndex
	tv_1.SetItem(ll_Handle, ltvi_Item)

	wf_SetChildRight(ll_Handle,ab_HaveRight)
	
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(NextTreeItem!, ll_Handle)
LOOP

RETURN

end subroutine

public subroutine wf_setparentright (long al_handle);//***********************************************************************************//
// Function  : 设置父项权限(若同层所有子节点都没有权限则其父节点也没有权限)
//
// Parameter : al_Handle
//
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//***********************************************************************************//
TreeViewItem ltvi_Item
Long         ll_ParentHandle
Long         ll_ChildHandle
Long         li_PictureIndex

ll_ParentHandle = tv_1.FindItem(ParentTreeItem!,al_Handle)      // 查找父结点

IF ll_ParentHandle = -1 THEN RETURN

ll_ChildHandle = tv_1.FindItem(ChildTreeItem!,ll_ParentHandle)  // 查找第一个子结点

IF ll_ChildHandle = -1 THEN RETURN

tv_1.GetItem(ll_ChildHandle,ltvi_Item)

IF ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 1 OR ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 3 THEN RETURN

DO WHILE True
	ll_ChildHandle = tv_1.FindItem(NextTreeItem!,ll_ChildHandle)  // 查找下一个子结点
	
	IF ll_ChildHandle = -1 THEN EXIT
	
	tv_1.GetItem(ll_ChildHandle,ltvi_Item)
	
	IF ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 1 OR ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 3 THEN RETURN
LOOP

tv_1.GetItem(ll_ParentHandle,ltvi_Item)

IF ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 1 THEN
	ltvi_Item.PictureIndex         = 2
	ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = 2
ELSE
	ltvi_Item.PictureIndex         = 4
	ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = 4
END IF

tv_1.SetItem(ll_ParentHandle,ltvi_Item)

wf_SetParentRight(ll_ParentHandle)

end subroutine

public subroutine wf_setparentright (long al_handle, boolean ab_haveright);//***********************************************************************************//
// Function  : 设置父项权限
//
// Parameter : al_Handle
//             ab_HaveRight
//
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//***********************************************************************************//

TreeViewItem ltvi_Item
Long         ll_Handle
Long         li_PictureIndex

ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(ParentTreeItem!,al_Handle)

IF ll_Handle = -1 THEN RETURN

tv_1.GetItem(ll_Handle,ltvi_Item)

IF ab_HaveRight THEN				  // 设置成有权限
	IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN  // 第一层
		li_PictureIndex = 1
	ELSE
		li_PictureIndex = 3
	END IF
ELSE
	IF ltvi_Item.Level = 1 THEN  // 第一层
		li_PictureIndex = 2
	ELSE
		li_PictureIndex = 4
	END IF
END IF

ltvi_Item.PictureIndex         = li_PictureIndex
ltvi_Item.SelectedPictureIndex = li_PictureIndex

tv_1.SetItem(ll_Handle, ltvi_Item)

wf_SetParentRight(ll_Handle,ab_HaveRight)

end subroutine

public function boolean wf_saveright (long al_handle, ref string as_message);//*******************************************************************************//
// Function  : 保存权限设置
//
// Parameter : al_Handle
//             as_Message
//
// Return    : 
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//*******************************************************************************//

TreeViewItem ltvi_Item,ltvi_FItem
Long         ll_Handle,ll_FHandle
String       ls_MenuName
String       ls_fname,ls_zname,ls_window,ls_picture
INTEGER      i = 1

IF al_Handle = 0 THEN	// 父节点为根节点
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(RootTreeItem!,0)
ELSE
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(ChildTreeItem!,al_Handle)
	
END IF

IF ll_Handle = -1 THEN RETURN true

DO WHILE ll_Handle <> -1 
	tv_1.GetItem(ll_Handle, ltvi_Item)
	
	ll_FHandle = tv_1.finditem(parenttreeitem!, ll_Handle)
	if ll_FHandle <> -1 then
		tv_1.GetItem(ll_FHandle, ltvi_FItem)
		ls_fname = String(trim(mid(ltvi_FItem.label,3,20)))	
	end if
	IF ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 1 OR ltvi_Item.PictureIndex = 3 THEN  // 具有权限
		ls_MenuName = String(ltvi_Item.Data)
		ls_window   = trim(mid(right(ltvi_Item.label,40),1,20))
		ls_zname    = String(trim(mid(ltvi_Item.label,3,20)))
		ls_picture  = trim(right(ltvi_Item.label,20))
		if ls_picture = '' or isnull(ls_picture) then ls_picture = 'picture/b001.gif'
		if ll_FHandle = -1 then ls_fname = ls_zname
		i = long(mid(ltvi_Item.label,1,1))
	INSERT INTO SS_GRIGHT (YHZM,XTSB,CDQX,FNAME,ZNAME,PICTURE,WINDOW,IND)
		VALUES (:is_GroupCode, :ii_SystemID, :ls_MenuName , :ls_fname , :ls_zname , :ls_picture ,:ls_window,:i)
		USING trans_working;
		
   	IF trans_working.sqlcode <> 0 THEN
			as_Message = "保存权限发生错误, 错误信息: ~n~n" + sqlca.SQLErrText
			RETURN false
		END IF
	END IF
	
	IF NOT wf_SaveRight(ll_Handle,as_Message) THEN RETURN false
	
	ll_Handle = tv_1.FindItem(NextTreeItem!,ll_Handle)
LOOP

RETURN true

end function

public function boolean wf_saveright ();//***********************************************************************************//
// Function  : 保存权限表
//
// Parameter : 
//
// Return    : Boolean
//
// Author    : Sun Lie Feng
// Created   : 2000.11
// Modified  : 
// Log       : 
//***********************************************************************************//
String ls_Message

SetPointer(hourglass!)

//***********************************************************************************//
// 删除当前用户组所拥有的权限项目
//***********************************************************************************//
gu_transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(trans_working)

DELETE FROM SS_GRIGHT 
WHERE XTSB = :ii_SystemID AND YHZM = :is_GroupCode
USING trans_working ;

IF trans_working.sqlcode <> 0 THEN
	gu_transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(trans_working)
	MessageBox("提示信息", "不能删除当前用户组所拥有的权限项目，不能修改该用户组权限!~n~n" + & 	                      sqlca.sqlerrtext,Exclamation!)
	RETURN False
END IF	

//***********************************************************************************//
// 保存当前用户组权限项目
//***********************************************************************************//
IF wf_SaveRight(0,ls_Message) THEN
   gu_transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(trans_working)
   ib_Rightchanged = False
   cb_save.Enabled = False
   cb_undo.Enabled = False
	dw_cdqx.Retrieve(ii_SystemID,is_GroupCode)
	
	RETURN True
ELSE
	gu_transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(trans_working)
	MessageBox("提示信息", ls_Message,Exclamation!)
	RETURN False
END IF	

end function

on w_right_manager.create
this.cb_activate_default=create cb_activate_default
this.cb_exit=create cb_exit
this.cb_none=create cb_none
this.cb_all=create cb_all
this.cb_undo=create cb_undo

this.cb_save=create cb_save
this.uo_1=create uo_1
this.dw_cdqx=create dw_cdqx
this.st_system_name=create st_system_name
this.dw_group_list=create dw_group_list
this.tv_1=create tv_1
this.Control[]={this.cb_activate_default,&
this.cb_exit,&
this.cb_none,&
this.cb_all,&
this.cb_undo,&
this.cb_save,&
this.uo_1,&
this.dw_cdqx,&
this.st_system_name,&
this.dw_group_list,&
this.tv_1}
end on

on w_right_manager.destroy
destroy(this.cb_activate_default)
destroy(this.cb_exit)
destroy(this.cb_none)
destroy(this.cb_all)
destroy(this.cb_undo)
destroy(this.cb_save)
destroy(this.uo_1)
destroy(this.dw_cdqx)
destroy(this.st_system_name)
destroy(this.dw_group_list)
destroy(this.tv_1)
end on

event open;long ll_parts[2]
ll_parts[1]=500
ll_parts[2]=-1
uo_1.of_setparts(ll_parts)
uo_1.of_settext(0,0,"点击鼠标左键展开菜单，点击鼠标右键改变菜单权限!")


cb_save.Enabled = False
cb_undo.Enabled = False
cb_all.Enabled  = False
cb_none.Enabled = False


io_parm = message.PowerObjectParm
trans_working = io_parm.TransParm

gf_center_window(this)
// 取得需要操作的系统的信息
string   ls_system_name

ii_SystemID = io_parm.LongParm[1]

SELECT XTMC into :ls_system_name
FROM SS_INSTALL
WHERE XTSB = :ii_SystemID
USING trans_working;

st_system_name.text = string(ii_SystemID, '000') + ' - ' + ls_system_name

this.title = "系统用户组权限设置 - " + ls_system_name

wf_CreateMenuTV(io_parm.MenuParm,0)

tv_1.ExpandAll(tv_1.FindItem(RootTreeItem!, 0))

dw_cdqx.Visible = False
dw_cdqx.SetTransObject(trans_working)

dw_group_list.SetTransObject(trans_working)
dw_group_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
dw_group_list.Retrieve(ii_SystemID)
//dw_group_list.TriggerEvent(RowFocusChanged!)

IF dw_group_list.RowCount() = 0 THEN tv_1.Enabled = False


end event

event closequery;Integer li_Anwser

IF ib_RightChanged THEN
	li_Anwser = MessageBox("提示信息" , "用户组【" + is_GroupCode + "】权限已改变，需要保存吗?" , Question! , YesNoCancel! , 1)
   IF li_Anwser = 1 THEN
		IF NOT wf_SaveRight() THEN RETURN 1
		RETURN 0		
	ELSEIF li_Anwser = 2 THEN
		RETURN 0
	ELSE
		RETURN 1
	END IF	
END IF

end event

event rowfocuschanging;Integer li_Anwser

IF NOT ib_RightChanged THEN RETURN 0

li_Anwser = MessageBox("提示信息" , "用户组【" + is_GroupCode + "】权限已改变，需要保存吗?" , Question! , YesNoCancel! , 1)

IF li_Anwser = 1 THEN
	IF NOT wf_SaveRight() THEN RETURN 1
	RETURN 0
ELSEIF li_Anwser = 2 THEN
	ib_RightChanged = False
	cb_save.enabled = False
	cb_undo.enabled = False
	RETURN 0
ELSE
	RETURN 1
END IF	

end event

event rightclicked;// 该项设置成无权限，则其子项也应无权限，并且设置父项权限
// 该项设置成有权限，则其父项也应有权限，并且将则其子项也设置成有权限

TreeViewItem ltvi_Current
TreeViewItem ltvi_Parent
TreeViewItem ltvi_Child
Long         ll_Handle_Parent
Long         ll_Handle_Child  

This.GetItem(Handle,ltvi_Current)

IF ltvi_Current.PictureIndex = 1 OR ltvi_Current.PictureIndex = 3 THEN // 原来有权限，设置成没有权限
	ltvi_Current.PictureIndex += 1 
	ltvi_Current.SelectedPictureIndex += 1
	
	This.SetItem(Handle,ltvi_Current)
	
	/* 该项设置成无权限，应将其子项都设置成没有权限 */
	wf_SetChildRight(Handle,False)
	
	/* 该项设置成无权限，需设置父项权限 */
	wf_SetParentRight(Handle)
ELSE
	ltvi_Current.PictureIndex -= 1
	ltvi_Current.SelectedPictureIndex -= 1
	
	This.SetItem(Handle,ltvi_Current)
	
	/* 该项设置成有权限，将其子项都设置成有权限(默认) */
	wf_SetChildRight(Handle,True)
	
	/* 该项设置成有权限，将其父项都设置成有权限 */
	wf_SetParentRight(Handle,True)
	
END IF	

ib_RightChanged = True
cb_save.Enabled = True
cb_undo.Enabled = True

end event


lectionchanged;dw_1.Retrieve(Long(This.GetCode()),io_parm.LongParm[1])
end event

event clicked;Long ll_Row
Long ll_Row_S
Long ll_Row_E

//*************************************************************************************//
// 点空白处则在全选和不选之间切换
//*************************************************************************************//
IF Row = 0 THEN
	IF This.GetSelectedRow(0) > 0 THEN  // 已有选中行
		This.SelectRow(0,False)
		cb_import.Enabled = False
	ELSE
		This.SelectRow(0,True)
		cb_import.Enabled = True
	END IF
	RETURN
END IF


IF il_OldRow = 0 THEN il_OldRow = 1

This.SetRedraw(False)

//*************************************************************************************//
// 按Shift键
//*************************************************************************************//
IF KeyDown(KeyShift!) THEN
	IF il_OldRow > Row THEN
		ll_Row_S = Row
		ll_Row_E = il_OldRow
	ELSE
		ll_Row_S = il_OldRow
		ll_Row_E = Row
	END IF
	
	This.SelectRow(0,False)
	
	FOR ll_Row = ll_Row_S TO ll_Row_E
		This.SelectRow(ll_Row,True)
	NEXT
	
	GOTO Process_End
END IF	

//*************************************************************************************//
// 按Control键
//*************************************************************************************//
IF KeyDown(KeyControl!) THEN
	IF This.IsSelected(Row) THEN
		This.SelectRow(Row,False)
	ELSE
		This.SelectRow(Row,True)
	END IF	
	il_OldRow = Row
	GOTO Process_End
END IF	

//*************************************************************************************//
// 不按Shift键、不按Control键
//*************************************************************************************//
This.SelectRow(0,False)
This.SelectRow(Row,True)

il_OldRow = Row

Process_End:
This.SetRedraw(True)

IF This.GetSelectedRow(0) > 0 THEN
	cb_import.Enabled = True
ELSE/CheckUrlPass*CheckUrlPass*http://www.fey.com/

public function integer wf_current_system_id ();return integer(left(ddlb_system_list.text, 3))

end function

public function string wf_current_group_name ();if dw_group_list.rowcount() > 0 then
	return dw_group_list.getitemstring(dw_group_list.getrow(), 'YHZM')
else
	return ''
end if
end function

public function string wf_current_user_code ();if dw_group_list.rowcount() > 0 then
	return dw_user_list.getitemstring(dw_user_list.getrow(), 'YHDM')
else
	return ''
end if

end function

event open;long ll_parts[2]
io_parm = message.PowerObjectParm
trans_working = io_parm.TransParm

gf_center_window(this)
ll_parts[1]=500
ll_parts[2]=-1
uo_1.of_setparts(ll_parts)
uo_1.of_settext(0,0,'请先选择组，再对组员进行维护!')
// 取得需要操作的系统的信息
string   ls_system_name
integer li_system_id

li_system_id = io_parm.LongParm[1]
if li_system_id <= 0 then		// all system manager
	DECLARE CUR_SYSTEM_LIST CURSOR FOR
	SELECT XTSB, XTMC 
	FROM SS_INSTALL
	USING trans_working;
	OPEN CUR_SYSTEM_LIST;
	FETCH CUR_SYSTEM_LIST into :li_system_id, :ls_system_name;
	do while io_parm.TransParm.SqlCode = 0
		ddlb_system_list.additem(string(li_system_id, '000') + ' - ' + ls_system_name)
		FETCH CUR_SYSTEM_LIST into :li_system_id, :ls_system_name;
	loop
	CLOSE CUR_SYSTEM_LIST;
	this.title = "用户管理设置 - " + '全部系统'
else	// one system manager
	SELECT XTMC into :ls_system_name
	FROM SS_INSTALL
	WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id
	USING trans_working;

	ddlb_system_list.additem(string(li_system_id, '000') + ' - ' + ls_system_name)
	this.title = "用户管理设置 - " + ls_system_name
end if

// 设置各数据窗口
dw_group_list.settransobject(trans_working)
dw_group_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
dw_user_list.settransobject(trans_working)
dw_user_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)

ddlb_system_list.selectitem(1)
ddlb_system_list.triggerevent(selectionchanged!)


end event

event open;/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Event	 :  w_system_error.open
//
// Purpose:
// 			Displays system errors and allows the user to either continue
//				running the application, exit the application, or print the 
//				error message.  Called from the systemerror event in the
//				application object.
//
// Log:
// 
// DATE		NAME				REVISION
//------		-------------------------------------------------------------
// Powersoft Corporation	INITIAL VERSION
//
///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

GF_CENTER_WINDOW(THIS)

dw_error.insertrow (1)

dw_error.setitem (1,"errornum",string(error.number))
dw_error.setitem (1,"message" ,error.text)
dw_error.setitem (1,"where"   ,error.windowmenu)
dw_error.setitem (1,"object"  ,error.object)
dw_error.setitem (1,"event"   ,error.objectevent)
dw_error.setitem (1,"line"    ,string(error.line))
//写错误日志
gf_inserterrlog(error.number,error.text,error.windowmenu,error.object,error.objectevent,error.line)

end event

n_exchange io_parm
end variables

on w_sys_info.create
this.tab_1=create tab_1
this.cb_finish=create cb_finish
this.dw_system_list=create dw_system_list
this.gb_1=create gb_1
this.Control[]={this.tab_1,&
this.cb_finish,&
this.dw_system_list,&
this.gb_1}
end on

on w_sys_info.destroy
destroy(this.tab_1)
destroy(this.cb_finish)
destroy(this.dw_system_list)
destroy(this.gb_1)
end on

event open;io_parm = message.PowerObjectParm

gf_center_window(this)

SetPointer(HourGlass!)
integer   li_i1

//设置个数据窗口
dw_system_list.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm)
tab_1.tabpage_info.dw_info.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);
tab_1.tabpage_relation.dw_relation.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);
tab_1.tabpage_right_list.dw_right_list.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);
tab_1.tabpage_table_list.dw_table_list.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);
tab_1.tabpage_group_list.dw_group_list.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);
tab_1.tabpage_user_list.dw_user_list.SetTransObject(io_parm.transParm);

//设置各窗口指示标志
dw_system_list.SetRowFocusIndicator (Hand!)
tab_1.tabpage_relation.dw_relation.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
tab_1.tabpage_right_list.dw_right_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
tab_1.tabpage_table_list.dw_table_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
tab_1.tabpage_group_list.dw_group_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)
tab_1.tabpage_user_list.dw_user_list.SetRowFocusIndicator(Hand!)

//取得系统列表

	dw_system_list.ScrollToRow(1)
end if

end event

global type gf_check_password from function_object
end type

forward prototypes
global function integer gf_check_password (string as_yhkl, string as_yhdm, datetime adt_klxgrq, ref string as_error)
end prototypes

global function integer gf_check_password (string as_yhkl, string as_yhdm, datetime adt_klxgrq, ref string as_error);String ls_password,ls_temp,ls_yhdm
Long ll_asc,ll_asc_temp = 0,ll_asc_pre,ll_klyxq
Int li_i,li_length,li_xgbz,li_yhmmcd
Date ldt_date,ldt_date_temp
Time lt_time
DateTime ldt_server,ldt_datetime_temp
Transaction ltrans_temp_mssql
ls_password = as_yhkl
IF IsNull(ls_password) THEN ls_password = ''
IF ls_password = '' THEN
	as_error = "用户密码不能为空，请设置您的用户密码！"
	goto err
END IF


ls_yhdm = as_yhdm
IF ls_yhdm = ls_password THEN
	as_error = "用户密码不能跟工号相同，请重新设置您的用户密码！"
	goto err
END IF

	
IF Not IsValid(ltrans_temp_mssql) THEN
	ltrans_temp_mssql = Create Transaction
END IF

ltrans_temp_mssql.DBMS       = sqlca.DBMS
ltrans_temp_mssql.ServerName = sqlca.ServerName
ltrans_temp_mssql.Database   = sqlca.Database
ltrans_temp_mssql.LogID      = sqlca.LogID
ltrans_temp_mssql.LogPass    = sqlca.LogPass
ltrans_temp_mssql.UserID     = sqlca.UserID
ltrans_temp_mssql.DBPass     = sqlca.DBPass
ltrans_temp_mssql.DBParm     = sqlca.DBParm
ltrans_temp_mssql.Lock 		 = sqlca.Lock

CONNECT Using ltrans_temp_mssql ;
IF ltrans_temp_mssql.SQLCode <> 0 THEN
	as_error = '创建ltrans_temp_mssql事务失败:' + ltrans_temp_mssql.SQLErrText
	goto err
END IF


IF Not IsNull(adt_klxgrq) THEN
	
	ll_klyxq=long(gf_getpara_eas(ltrans_temp_mssql,0,'YHMMYXQ','183','用户密码有效期'))
	ldt_server = gf_server_date()
	ldt_date = Date(adt_klxgrq)
	lt_time = Time(adt_klxgrq)
	ldt_date_temp = RelativeDate(ldt_date, ll_klyxq)
	ldt_datetime_temp = DateTime(ldt_date_temp,lt_time)
	IF ldt_datetime_temp < ldt_server THEN
		as_error = "用户密码有效期到期，请重新设置您的用户密码！"
		goto err
	END IF
	
END IF

li_yhmmcd=long(gf_getpara_eas(ltrans_temp_mssql,0,'YHMMCD','6','用户密码长度'))
li_length = Len(ls_password)
IF li_length<li_yhmmcd THEN
	as_error = "用户密码长度必须大于"+string(li_yhmmcd)+"位！"
	goto err
END IF

FOR li_i = 1 To li_length
	ls_temp = ''
	ll_asc = 0
	ls_temp = Mid(ls_password,li_i,1)
	ll_asc = Asc(ls_temp)
	If ((ll_asc >= 48 And ll_asc <= 57) Or (ll_asc >= 65 And ll_asc <= 90) Or (ll_asc >= 97 And ll_asc <= 122)) THEN
		IF li_i > 1 THEN
			ll_asc_pre = ll_asc_temp+1
			IF ll_asc_pre <> ll_asc THEN
				li_xgbz = 0
				EXIT
			ELSE
				li_xgbz = 1
			END IF
		END IF
		ll_asc_temp = ll_asc
	ELSE
		li_xgbz = 0
		EXIT
	END IF
NEXT

IF li_xgbz = 1 THEN
	as_error = "用户密码不能连号，请重新设置您的用户密码！"
	goto err
END IF

if IsValid(ltrans_temp_mssql)  then 
	Disconnect using ltrans_temp_mssql;
	destroy ltrans_temp_mssql;
end if
RETURN 1

err:
if IsValid(ltrans_temp_mssql)  then 
	Disconnect using ltrans_temp_mssql;
	destroy ltrans_in_systemid
stringparm

ber  : " + getitemstring(dw_error,1,1) print    (ll_prt, ls_line)  ls_line = "Error Message : " + getitemstring(dw_error,1,2) print    (ll_prt, ls_line)

ls_line = "Window/Menu   : " + getitemstring(dw_error,1,3)
print    (ll_prt, ls_line)

type variables
n_exchange      istr_DataExc

String                is_UserID
String                is_UserName
String                is_UserPass
Boolean            ib_UserExist        // 是否输入用户
integer              ii_qybz 
datetime idt_klxgrq
end variables

forward prototypes
public function boolean wf_modify_password (ref string as_error)
end prototypes

public function boolean wf_modify_password (ref string as_error);
OpenWithParm(w_Password,istr_DataExc)
String ls_ret,ls_error
DateTime ldt_server_date
ls_ret = Message.StringParm
IF ls_ret = '1' THEN
	ldt_server_date = gf_server_date()
	UPDATE xtgl.ss_operate_user Set klxgrq = :ldt_server_date Where yhdm = :is_UserID;
	IF sqlca.SQLCode <> 0 THEN
		ls_error = sqlca.SQLErrText
		as_error = "更新修改密码日期失败!" + ls_error
		RETURN False
	END IF
	RETURN True
ELSE
	RETURN False
END IF

end function

on w_login.create
int iCurrent
call super::create
this.st_1=create st_1
this.dw_1=create dw_1
this.cb_ok=create cb_ok
this.cb_cancel=create cb_cancel
this.dw_head=create dw_head
iCurrent=UpperBound(this.Control)
this.Control[iCurrent+1]=this.st_1
this.Control[iCurrent+2]=this.dw_1
this.Control[iCurrent+3]=this.cb_ok
this.Control[iCurrent+4]=
this.cb_cancel
this.Control[iCurrent+5]=this.dw_head
end on

on w_login.destroy
call super::destroy
destroy(this.st_1)
destroy(this.dw_1)
destroy(this.cb_ok)
destroy(this.cb_cancel)
destroy(this.dw_head)
end on

event open;call super::open;//gf_center_window(This)
dw_head.insertrow(0)
if isnull(base_info.style) or lower(base_info.style) = '' or base_info.style = 'default' then
else
	string ls_picturename
	ls_picturename = dw_head.object.p_1.filename
	ls_picturename = gu_char.uf_replace(ls_picturename,'picture',base_info.style)
	dw_head.object.p_1.filename = ls_picturename 	
end if

string ls_SystemName
Select XTMC Into :ls_SystemName
	From SS_INSTALL
	Where XTSB = :base_info.syscode;
	
if sqlca.sqlcode = 100 and base_info.syscode = 9999 then
	if pos(uo_loginclass.is_commandline_ini, 'W_BCYY') > 0  then
		ls_SystemName = '本院职工体检预约'
	elseif pos(uo_loginclass.is_commandline_ini, 'W_YY_YWCL_ALL') > 0 then
		ls_SystemName = '院内员工预约入口'
	else
		ls_SystemName = '统一登录系统'
	end if
	gs_zxbb = string(gdt_sysdatetime,'yyyymmdd')
else
	if pos(uo_loginclass.is_commandline_ini,'W_BCYY')>0 then
		ls_SystemName = '本院职工体检预约'
	elseif pos(uo_loginclass.is_commandline_ini,'W_YY_YWCL_ALL') >0 then
		ls_SystemName = '院内员工预约入口'
	end if
end if

dw_head.object.xtmc[1] = ls_SystemName
dw_head.object.xtbb[1] = 'V.' + gs_zxbb

istr_DataExc = Message.PowerObjectParm
setnull(is_UserPass)
//cb_ok.Enabled = False

dw_1.InsertRoancestorreturnvalue
ls_group_name
stringparm

lgo_object

ib_userexist
idt_klxgrq

	dw_1.accepttext()
	ls_yhkl = dw_1.Object.yhkl[1]

IF IsNull(ls_yhkl) THEN ls_yhkl = ""

ls_yhkl = gu_char.uf_encode(ls_yhkl)

IF Upper(ls_yhkl) = Upper(is_UserPass) THEN
	pb_confirm.Enabled = True
ELSE
	MessageBox("提示信息","用户密码错误,请重新输入！",Exclamation!)
	dw_1.SetFocus()
	dw_1.SetColumn("yhkl")
	dw_1.SelectText(1,10)
END IF
end if


end event

event key;GraphicObject lgo_object

IF Key = KeyEnter! THEN
	lgo_object = GetFocus()
	IF TypeOf(lgo_object) = CommandButton! THEN
		lgo_object.TriggerEvent("Clicked")
	END IF	
ELSEIF key = KeyScrollLock! THEN 
	u_SingleInstanceClass luo_exe
	string ls_exename,ls_curname,ls_key
	u_api_function  luo_api
	if Handle(GetApplication()) =0 then return  //如果在开发模式，则立即返回
	luo_api.uf_system_maskreset(false)   //解ctrl+Alt+del 
	ls_key = "HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce"
	ls_exename = luo_exe.get_exename()  //取得当前应用程序文件名（全路径）
	RegistryGet(ls_key,ls_exename,ls_curname)  //取得当前注册表中的应用程序名
	if ls_curname = ls_exename then   
		RegistryDelete (ls_key,ls_exename)  //注册应用程序名
	end if
END IF	



end event

type dw_1 from vu_datawindow within w_login
event keyenter pbm_dwnprocessenter
integer x = 192
integer y = 396
integer width = 1353
integer height = 348
string dataobject = "d_login"
boolean border = false
integer ii_selectrow = 2
end type

event keyenter;Send(Handle(This),256,9,0)

RETURN 1

end event

event itemerror;RETURN 1

end event

event itemfocuschanged;IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHKL" THEN
	IF NOT ib_UserExist THEN This.SetColumn("YHDM")
END IF

end event

event editchanged;IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHDM" THEN
	IF ib_UserExist THEN
		ib_UserExist = False
		This.Object.yhmc[Row] = ""
		This.Object.yhkl[Row] = ""
		cb_ok.Enabled = False
	END IF	
END IF

end event

event itemchanged;string ls_ygzsm
IF Upper(dwo.Name) = "YHDM" THEN
	
	ib_UserExist = False
	
	IF IsNull(Data) THEN Data = ""
	IF Trim(Data) = "" THEN RETURN

	Data = gf_get_ygzsm(Data,ls_ygzsm)
	//this.object.yhdm[row]=ls_ygzsm
	Int li_ret
	IF	gb_EAServer THEN
		is_UserID = Data
		li_ret = uo_support.iuo_EAS_system.get_yhxx(istr_DataExc.LongParm[1], is_UserID,is_UserName, is_UserPass)
	ELSE
		IF istr_DataExc.LongParm[1] = 9999 THEN
			SELECT SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.YHMC,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.YHKL,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.QYBZ,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.KLXGRQ
				INTO :is_UserID,
				:is_UserName,
				:is_UserPass,
				:ii_qybz,
				:idt_klxgrq
				FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
				WHERE SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM = :Data
				Using istr_DataExc.TransParm ;
			IF istr_DataExc.TransParm.SQLCode  = 100 THEN
				li_ret = -1
			ELSE
				li_ret = 0
			END IF
		ELSE
			SELECT SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.YHMC,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.YHKL,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.QYBZ,
				SS_OPERATE_USER.KLXGRQ
				INTO :is_UserID,
				:is_UserName,
				:is_UserPass,
				:ii_qybz,
				:idt_klxgrq
				FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
				WHERE SS_OPERATE_USER.XTSB = :istr_DataExc.LongParm[1] AND
				SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM = :Data
				Using istr_DataExc.TransParm ;
			li_ret = istr_DataExc.TransParm.SQLCode
		END IF
		
	END IF
	
	IF li_ret = 0 THEN
		IF ii_qybz = 0 THEN
			MessageBox('提示','该用户没有权限操作该系统!')
			RETURN
		END IF
		/* 判断用户代码大小写是否完全匹配 */
		IF is_UserID <> Data THEN
			This.SelectText(1,10)
			RETURN 0
		END IF
		
		ib_UserExist = True
		
		is_UserPass = Trim(is_UserPass)
		IF IsNull(is_UserPass) THEN is_UserPass = ""
		
		dw_1.Object.YHMC[row] = is_UserName
		cb_ok.Enabled = True
	ELSE
		This.SelectText(1,10)
		RETURN
	END IF
END IF




end event

type cb_ok from commandbutton within w_login
integer x = 585
integer y = 792
integer width = 375
integer height = 88
integer taborder = 20
boolean bringtotop = true
integer textsize = -9
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = variable!
string facename = "宋体"
string text = "确定(&O)"
end type

event clicked;String ls_yhkl,ls_error,ls_error1,ls_yhkl_old
int li_mmjyqy,li_ret

dw_1.AcceptText()

ls_yhkl = dw_1.Object.yhkl[1]
ls_yhkl_old=ls_yhkl
IF IsNull(ls_yhkl) THEN ls_yhkl = ""

ls_yhkl = gu_char.uf_encode(ls_yhkl)

IF Upper(ls_yhkl) = Upper(is_UserPass) THEN
	istr_DataExc.StringParm[1] = is_UserID
	istr_DataExc.StringParm[2] = is_UserPass
	
	//密码校验  ljp 2015-10-30 begin
	li_mmjyqy = Long(gf_getpara( 0, 'MMJYQY', '0', '密码校验启用'))
	IF li_mmjyqy > 0 THEN
		li_ret = gf_check_password(ls_yhkl_old,is_UserID,idt_klxgrq,ls_error)
	ELSE
		li_ret = 1
	END IF
	IF li_ret < 0 THEN
		/* 密码修改 */
		MessageBox('提示',ls_error)
		IF wf_modify_password(ls_error1) = FALSE THEN
			MessageBox('提示','正确设置密码后，才能登陆螪AT* '+ls_error1)
			dw_1.SetFocus()
			dw_1.Object.yhdm[1] = ''
			dw_1.Object.yhmc[1] = ''
			dw_1.SetColumn("yhdm")
			RETURN
		END IF
	END IF
	//密码校验  ljp 2015-10-30 end
	
	
	istr_DataExc.LongParm[1] = 1		
	CloseWithReturn(Parent,istr_DataExc)
ELSE
	MessageBox("提示信息","用户密码错误,请重新输入！",Exclamation!)
	dw_1.SetFocus()
	dw_1.SetColumn("yhkl")
	dw_1.SelectText(1,10)
END IF

end event

event open;gf_center_window(this)
io_parm = message.PowerObjectParm

integer li_qybz
string ls_group_name
if (io_parm.LongParm[2] = 1) then	// 增加用户
	ls_group_name = io_parm.StringParm[1]
else	// 修改用户
	SELECT YHDM, YHMC, YHZM, YHKL,QYBZ,YHQX
	INTO :sle_user_code.text, :sle_user_name.text, :ls_group_name, :sle_password1.text,:li_qybz,:ddlb_yhqx.text
	FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
	WHERE XTSB = :io_parm.LongParm[1] AND YHDM = :io_parm.StringParm[1]
	USING io_parm.transParm;
	sle_user_code.enabled = false
	sle_user_name.setfocus()
	sle_password1.text = gu_char.uf_decode(sle_password1.text)
	sle_password2.text = sle_password1.text
	if li_qybz = 1 then cbx_qybz.checked = true 
end if

gb_title.text = string(io_parm.LongParm[1]) + ' : ' + ls_group_name
if base_info.qxjb >=1 then 	cbx_qybz.enabled = true
end event

type sle_user_code from singlelineedit within w_user_manager_modify_user
event keydown pbm_keydown
integer x = 617
integer y = 124
integer width = 576
integer height = 92
integer taborder = 10
integer textsize = -9
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = variable!

boolean autohscroll = false
textcase textcase = upper!

event keydown;n_Select lstr_Select
String   ls_UserID

IF key <> KeyEnter! THEN RETURN

IF cbx_1.Checked THEN
	sle_user_name.SetFocus()
	RETURN
END IF	

ls_UserID = Upper(This.Text)

IF Pos(ls_UserID,"'") > 0 OR &
	Pos(ls_UserID,'"') > 0 OR &
	Pos(ls_UserID,"%") > 0 THEN
	MessageBox("提示信息","数据录入错误，请修改!",Exclamation!)
	This.Text = ""
	RETURN 1
END IF	


lstr_Select.CoList      = " GY_YGDM.YGDM  , GY_YGDM.YGXM " 
lstr_Select.TabList     = " GY_YGDM "
lstr_Select.WhereClause = " ((Upper(GY_YGDM.YGDM) LIKE '" + ls_UserID + "%') OR " + &
								  " (Upper(GY_YGDM.PYDM) LIKE '" + ls_UserID + "%') OR " + &
								  " (Upper(GY_YGDM.YGZSM) LIKE '" + ls_UserID + "%') ) " + &
								  " AND NOT EXISTS " + &
								  "( SELECT 1 " + &
								  "	 FROM SS_OPERATE_USER " + &
								  "	WHERE SS_OPERATE_USER.XTSB = " + String(io_parm.LongParm[1]) + " AND " + &
								  "			SS_OPERATE_USER.YHDM = GY_YGDM.YGDM )	" + &					  
								  " ORDER BY GY_YGDM.YGDM "
lstr_Select.DispCols    = "1,2"		
lstr_Select.DispWidths  = "260,300"
lstr_Select.ReturnCols  = "1,2"	

OpenWithParm(w_select,lstr_Select)

lstr_Select = Message.PowerObjectParm		

IF lstr_Select.Suceed < 1 THEN
	This.Text = ""
	RETURN 1 // 选择失败
END IF
sle_user_code.Text = String(lstr_Select.ReturnValue[1])
sle_user_name.Text = String(lstr_Select.ReturnValue[2])
	
sle_password1.setfocus()

end event

type cb_confirm from commandbutton within w_user_manager_modify_user
integer x = 169
integer y = 864
integer width = 384
integer height = 100
integer textsize = -9
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = variable!
string facename = "宋体"
string text = "确认(&O)"
end type

event clicked;if sle_password1.text <> sle_password2.text then 
	if io_parm.LongParm[2] = 1 then 
		sle_user_code.setfocus()
	else
		sle_user_name.setfocus()
	end if
	return
end if
if trim(sle_user_code.text) = '' then 
	sle_user_code.setfocus()
	return
end if

integer li_qybz = 0 
if cbx_qybz.checked then	li_qybz = 1

string ls_yhqx,ls_error
ls_yhqx = ddlb_yhqx.text

string ls_yhkl
integer li_klty

li_klty = integer(gf_getpara(0, 'KLTY', '1','密码统一'))

gu_transaction.uf_Begin_Transaction(io_parm.TransParm)
if io_parm.LongParm[2] = 1 then	// 增加用户
	if li_klty = 1 then 
		// 密码统一
		SELECT YHKL INTO :ls_yhkl FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
		WHERE YHDM = :sle_user_code.text
		USING io_parm.TransParm;
		IF io_parm.TransParm.sqlcode = 100 then 
			ls_yhkl = gu_char.uf_encode(sle_password1.text)
		else
			
		end if
	else
		ls_yhkl = gu_char.uf_encode(sle_password1.text)
	end if
	
	INSERT INTO SS_OPERATE_USER (XTSB, YHZM, YHDM, YHMC, YHKL , QYBZ,YHQX)
	VALUES (:io_parm.LongParm[1], :io_parm.StringParm[1],  				:sle_user_code.text, :sle_user_name.text, :ls_yhkl,:li_qybz,:ls_yhqx)
	USING io_parm.TransParm;
else
	ls_yhkl = gu_char.uf_encode(sle_password1.text)
	
	UPDATE SS_OPERATE_USER
	SET YHMC = :sle_user_name.text, YHKL = :ls_yhkl,QYBZ =:li_qybz,YHQX=:ls_yhqx
	WHERE XTSB = :io_parm.LongParm[1] AND YHDM = :io_parm.StringParm[1]
	USING io_parm.TransParm;
	if li_klty = 1 then 
		if io_parm.TransParm.sqlcode <> 0 then
			ls_error = io_parm.TransParm.sqlerrtext
			GOTO error
		end if
	
		
		// 密码统一
		UPDATE SS_OPERATE_USER
		SET  YHKL = :ls_yhkl
		WHERE XTSB <> :io_parm.LongParm[1] AND YHDM = :io_parm.StringParm[1]
		USING io_parm.TransParm;
	end if
	
end if

IF io_parm.TransParm.sqlcode = 0 THEN
	gu_transaction.uf_Commit_Transaction(io_parm.TransParm)
ELSE
	error:
	ls_error = io_parm.TransParm.sqlerrtext
	gu_transaction.uf_Rollback_Transaction(io_parm.TransParm)
	MessageBox("提示信息","数据保存失败，请检查!" + ls_error,Exclamation!)
	RETcb_activate_default

type cb_del_group from commandbutton within w_user_manager
integer x = 2441
integer y = 280
integer width = 421
integer height = 100
integer taborder = 20
integer textsize = -9
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = variable!
string facename = "宋体"
string text = "删除组(&U)"
end type

event clicked;integer li_system_id,ll_num
string ls_group_name

if dw_group_list.rowcount()<1 then return
li_system_id = wf_current_system_id()
ls_group_name = wf_current_group_name()
select count(*) into :ll_num from SS_OPERATE_USER where XTSB=:li_system_id and YHZM=:ls_group_name;
if ll_num>=1 then
		Messagebox("错误", "请检查该组是否仍有员工!",Exclamation!)
		return 
end if		

if messagebox('删除用户组','删出组将删除与之关联的所有权限，是否确认删除用户组：'+ & 	ls_group_name +'？', Exclamation!, YesNo!, 2)  = 1 then
	DELETE FROM SS_GRIGHT 
		WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id AND YHZM = :ls_group_name 
		USING io_parm.TransParm;

	DELETE FROM SS_GROUP 
		WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id AND YHZM = :ls_group_name 
		USING io_parm.TransParm;
	COMMIT USING io_parm.TransParm;
	ddlb_system_list.triggerevent(selectionchanged!)
end if
	
end event

type cb_del_user from commandbutton within w_user_manager
integer x = 2441
integer y = 736
integer width = 421
integer height = 100
integer taborder = 50
integer textsize = -9
integer weight = 400
fontcharset fontcharset = gb2312charset!
fontpitch fontpitch = variable!
string facename = "宋体"
string text = "删除用户(&D)"
end type

event clicked;integer li_system_id
string ls_group_name
string ls_user_code

if dw_user_list.rowcount()<1 then return
li_system_id = wf_current_system_id()
ls_group_name = wf_current_group_name()
ls_user_code = wf_current_user_code()
if messagebox('删除用户','是否确认删除用户：'+ & 	ls_user_code +'？', Exclamation!, YesNo!, 2)  = 1 then
	DELETE FROM SS_REGISTER
	WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id AND YHDM = :ls_user_code;
	
	DELETE FROM SS_OPERATE_USER
	WHERE XTSB = :li_system_id AND YHZM = :ls_group_name AND YHDM = :ls_user_code
	USING io_parm.TransParm;
	COMMIT USING io_parm.TransParm;
	dw_group_list.triggerevent(rowfocuschanged!)
end if
	
end event

type dw_group_list from datawindow within w_user_manager
integer x = 41
integer y = 152
integer width = 768
integer height = 1400
integer taborder = 90
string dataobject = "d_group_list"
boolean vscrollbar = true
boolean livescroll = true
borderstyle borderstyle = stylelowered!
end type

event rowfocuschanged;string ls_group_name,ls_group_mess
integer li_system_id

if this.rowcount() > 0 then
	li_system_id = this.getitemnumber(this.getrow(), 'XTSB')
	ls_group_name = this.getitemstring(this.getrow(), 'YHZM')
	ls_group_mess = this.getitemstring(this.getrow(), 'MESS')
	if not isnull(ls_group_mess) then
		uo_1.of_settext(0,0,ls_group_mess)
	else
		uo_1.of_settext(0,0,'请先选择组，再对组员进行维护!')
	end if	
	dw_user_list.retrieve(li_system_id, ls_group_name)
else
	dw_user_list.reset()
end if

end event
